### [99] [Ghosts that Connect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27297)
*Henrique Gomes*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将鬼场重新解释为主丛联络ω，同时消解了Faddeev–Popov程序中的两个概念难题：鬼场编码了丛的垂直结构，BRST对称性则是保持跨轨道配对的刚性垂直对称性，因此它能在规范固定后幸存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Faddeev–Popov方法存在两个概念性困惑：(1) 若规范等价位形代表相同物理，为何还需要鬼场这种在商空间上没有对应物的反交换辅助场？(2) 规范固定本应消除定域规范对称性，为何BRST对称性仍得以保留？这两个问题长期缺乏统一、几何清晰的解答。

Method: 作者遵循Dougherty (2021) 与 Dougherty & Read (2026) 的思路，将鬼场视为编码F→F/G丛上经典内容的工具，但识别出一种不同的结构：该丛上的主联络ω。具体而言，鬼场即为主联络ω，BRST算子为场空间上的竖直外微分，Maurer-Cartan方程为其竖直Cartan结构方程。Faddeev–Popov运算仅利用了ω的竖直分量（代数解读亦可捕捉），而ω的水平分量——Vilkovisky–DeWitt方案赖以建立的基础——提供了规范固定、 dressing量子化及反事实比较所需的跨轨道配对，这是商空间所丢弃的信息。

Result: 两个谜题同时消解：鬼场编码了F→F/G主丛的联络结构，BRST对称性之所以在规范固定后仍存在，是因为它是保持跨轨道配对的刚性竖直对称性。这一几何视角统一了Faddeev–Popov方法（竖直部分）与Vilkovisky–DeWitt方案（水平部分）。

Conclusion: 通过将Faddeev–Popov鬼场几何地解释为规范轨道丛上的主联络，本文提供了一个统一框架，既澄清了鬼场的物理来源，又解释了BRST对称性免于规范固定消除的原因，同时揭示了Faddeev–Popov方法与Vilkovisky–DeWitt几何方案之间的内在联系。

Abstract: The Faddeev--Popov procedure poses two conceptual puzzles. \emph{Puzzle~(1)}: if gauge-equivalent configurations represent the same physics, the quotient $\F/\G$ should suffice to compute physical amplitudes --- yet the procedure requires anti-commuting auxiliary fields, the ghosts, with no analogue on the quotient. What structure of $\F$ do they encode? \emph{Puzzle~(2)}: gauge-fixing was supposed to eliminate local gauge symmetry, yet the gauge-fixed theory retains BRST --- a residual symmetry that acts on the gauge potential as an infinitesimal gauge transformation. Why does it survive?
  Both puzzles dissolve together. Following \textcite{Dougherty2021} and \textcite{DoughertyRead2026}, I take ghosts to encode classical content of $\F \to \F/\G$, but identify a different structure: a principal connection $\varpi$ on this bundle. The ghost is $\varpi$; the BRST operator is the vertical exterior derivative on field space; the Maurer--Cartan equation is its vertical Cartan structure equation. The Faddeev--Popov calculus draws only on $\varpi$'s vertical content, which the algebraic reading also captures; $\varpi$'s horizontal content, on which the Vilkovisky--DeWitt programme rests, supplies the cross-orbit pairing that gauge-fixing, dressing-based quantisation, and counterfactual comparison require and the quotient discards. BRST is the rigid, vertical symmetry that preserves this pairing; this is why it survives gauge-fixing.

</details>

### [100] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for 2D Plane Wave Scattering in Arbitrary Dielectric Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27349)
*Zheng-Yu Huang,Yu Tian,Jing-Wen Zhang,Nicolae C. Panoiu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无网格物理信息神经网络的计算框架，用于求解二维非均匀介质中的电磁波散射问题。该框架将频域麦克斯韦方程和辐射边界条件嵌入神经网络损失函数，并通过在介质边界引入双曲正切平滑函数解决了TE极化下的奇异性问题，实现了对多种介质构型的高精度散射场预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法在求解非均匀介质中的电磁波散射问题时，面临网格生成复杂、处理不规则几何结构困难等挑战。本文旨在利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）的无网格特性，构建一个能够准确、鲁棒地求解二维非均匀介质电磁散射问题的统一计算框架。

Method: 作者提出了一种基于PINN的无网格计算框架：1）将频域麦克斯韦方程和辐射边界条件直接嵌入神经网络的损失函数中；2）分别处理TM和TE两种极化模式；3）针对TE极化下介质介电特性剧烈变化导致的控制方程奇异性问题，在介质边界处引入双曲正切平滑函数来平滑过渡，从而恢复求解精度。

Result: 该方法在单圆柱、同心多层圆柱壳、三任意排列圆柱及复合不规则结构等多种构型上进行了验证。TM极化下，所有相对L²误差均保持在≤0.1的极低水平。TE极化下，引入双曲正切平滑函数后，精度显著提升，结果与时域有限差分法（FDTD）的预测高度吻合。该框架在所有测试构型中均表现出稳定的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该PINN框架能够有效求解二维非均匀介质电磁散射问题，展现出良好的鲁棒性和对复杂电磁散射问题的可扩展性。双曲正切平滑函数是解决TE极化下介质边界奇异性问题的关键技术手段。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a meshless physics-informed neural network based computational framework for solving two-dimensional electromagnetic wave scattering in inhomogeneous media. The framework embeds frequency-domain Maxwell equations and radiation boundary conditions directly into the neural network loss function, enabling accurate prediction of scattered fields for both transverse magnetic (TM) and transverse electric (TE) polarizations across various dielectric configurations. Application of the method to single-cylinder, concentric multilayer cylindrical shells, three arbitrarily arranged cylinders, and composite irregular structures demonstrates that for the TM polarization, all relative $L^{2}$ errors mostly remain at particularly low levels of $\le0.1$. For the TE polarization, sharp variations of the dielectric properties of scatterers lead to singularities in the governing equations, which result in decreased accuracy of the method. This challenge is overcome by introducing at dielectric boundaries a hyperbolic-tangent smoothing function. This procedure significantly improves the accuracy of the method, with the corresponding results closely matching the predictions of the finite-difference time-domain method. This framework exhibits stable convergence behavior across all of the investigated configurations, thus confirming its robustness and scalability to complex electromagnetic scattering problems.

</details>


### [101] [Large-Time-Step Operation in a Volume Integral Equation for Dielectric Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28309)
*Sushil Kumar,Giampiero Gerini,M. C. van Beurden*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种时域电流密度体积分方程（MOT-JVIE）求解器在大时间步长下的运行性能，突破了传统CFL条件限制，时间步长可达参考值的16倍。研究发现大时间步长下计算瓶颈从历史项评估转移至当前时刻矩阵-向量乘积，并采用基于FFT的无矩阵策略利用多层Toeplitz结构解决了该瓶颈，在1500万未知量规模上实现了超一个数量级的计算成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式时域求解器受CFL条件限制，在处理精细离散化的介电散射问题时计算代价高昂。作者旨在探索MOT-JVIE求解器在远超CFL限制的大时间步长下进行精确瞬态分析的可行性，并解决由此引发的计算瓶颈问题。

Method: 采用时间步进（MOT）时域电流密度体积分方程（JVIE）求解器进行介电散射分析。分析了大时间步长下当前时刻因果相互作用区域扩张带来的计算特性转变。针对当前时刻相互作用矩阵的瓶颈，提出利用格林函数相关体积积分算子的多层Toeplitz结构，采用基于FFT的无矩阵矩阵-向量乘积策略。

Result: （1）在带限激励下，实现了高达16倍于CFL限制参考时间步长的精确瞬态分析；（2）揭示了大时间步长下主导计算成本从历史项评估向当前时刻矩阵-向量乘积的转变；（3）通过非均匀介电立方体和8×8纳米柱阵列（多尺度超表面结构）验证，计算成本降低超一个数量级；（4）在单线程CPU上成功演示了1560万未知量的大规模MOT-JVIE求解。

Conclusion: 大时间步长MOT-JVIE方案能够显著突破CFL条件限制，但会引发计算瓶颈的根本性转移。利用多层Toeplitz结构的FFT无矩阵策略可有效解决该瓶颈，使方法在单线程下即可处理超1500万未知量的大规模多尺度介电散射问题，大幅降低计算成本。

Abstract: In transient electromagnetic analysis, explicit time-domain solvers are restricted by the Courant-Friedrichs-Lewy (CFL) condition, making finely discretized dielectric scattering problems computationally expensive. This work investigates large-time-step operation in a marching-on-in-time time-domain current-density volume integral equation (MOT-JVIE) solver for dielectric scattering. For the considered band-limited excitations, accurate transient analysis is demonstrated for time steps up to 16 times larger than the reference CFL-limited time step associated with the voxel discretization. The study reveals a fundamental computational shift in the large-time-step regime. As the time-step size increases, the present-time causal interaction region expands, increasing the number of nonzero entries in the present-time interaction matrix and causing the dominant computational cost to transition from history-term evaluations to repeated matrix--vector products involving this matrix. Consequently, the present-time interaction matrix emerges as the principal scalability bottleneck in the large-time-step regime. To address this bottleneck, a matrix-free FFT-based matrix--vector-product strategy that exploits the multilevel Toeplitz structure of the Green-function-related volume-integral operator is employed for the present-time interaction matrix. The proposed framework is evaluated through an inhomogeneous dielectric cube and an 8 X 8 array of inhomogeneous dielectric nanopillars representative of multiscale metasurface structures, demonstrating more than an order-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost. In single-threaded execution, the method is demonstrated for 15.6 million unknowns, providing a large-scale MOT-JVIE demonstration beyond 15 million unknowns on one CPU thread.

</details>


### [102] [Frequency-Domain Analysis of Wave Scattering by Spatially Dispersive Metasurfaces Using the Method of Auxiliary Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28558)
*Minas Kouroublakis,Nikolaos L. Tsitsas,Yehuda Leviatan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将扩展的广义薄片跃变条件（GSTCs）与辅助源法（MAS）相结合，提出了一种无网格仿真框架，用于分析具有空间色散特性的平面、多边形、圆柱形等多种几何形状的超表面，并通过洛伦兹型空间谐振器的数值算例验证了方法的准确性和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间色散超表面具有角度依赖的响应，传统局部磁化率模型无法精确建模。扩展GSTCs虽能通过引入电磁场空间导数来考虑空间色散，但缺乏将其有效嵌入数值求解框架的便捷方法。因此需要一种无网格、灵活且精确的数值仿真框架。

Method: 将扩展的广义薄片跃变条件（GSTCs）集成到辅助源法（MAS）中，通过合理放置辅助源来施加扩展GSTCs。该无网格框架适用于无限大平面、有限平面、多边形及圆柱形超表面，并兼容双各向异性空间色散超表面。验证采用洛伦兹型空间谐振器。

Result: 针对平面、多边形、半圆形及圆柱形等多种几何形状的超表面给出了数值结果，与已发表数据高度吻合，证明了方法的准确性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 所提出的MAS-GSTCs联合框架能够精确、灵活地分析空间色散超表面，避免了传统网格方法的限制，且适用于多种几何构型和材料类型。

Abstract: Spatially dispersive metasurfaces exhibit angle-dependent responses that cannot be accurately modeled using conventional local susceptibilities. Extended Generalized Sheet Transition Conditions (GSTCs) have been introduced to account for spatial dispersion by incorporating spatial derivatives of the electromagnetic fields. In this work, these extended GSTCs are integrated into the Method of Auxiliary Sources (MAS), resulting in a meshless simulation framework for the analysis of spatially dispersive metasurfaces. The proposed formulation is developed for infinite planar, finite planar, polygon shaped, and cylindrical metasurfaces, while it is general and also applicable to bianisotropic spatially dispersive metasurfaces. For validation, the numerical examples consider Lorentz-type spatial resonators, consistent with previously published studies. The extended GSTCs are enforced within the MAS via appropriate placement of auxiliary sources. Numerical results are presented for several geometries, including planar, polygonal, semicircular, and cylindrical metasurfaces. The obtained results show very good agreement with previously published data, demonstrating the accuracy and flexibility of the proposed method.

</details>


### [103] [Brewster-anomaly delocalization for free-electron radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27894)
*Zheng Gong,Xiangfeng Xi,Hongsheng Chen,Owen D. Miller,Stefan Rotter,Xiao Lin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文揭示了布儒斯特异常退局域化与自由电子辐射之间的非常规相互作用，使得在强无序、非工程化多层介质中也能产生无局域化、高强度且定向的自由电子辐射，且该辐射对随机介质构型、光频率和电子速度均表现出显著的不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域化效应在无序电子学和光子学中至关重要，但它在自由电子-光相互作用中的作用（对激光器、加速器、显微镜、光谱学和量子信息等领域至关重要）仍未得到充分探索。同时，局域化效应被广泛认为是限制自由电子与随机介质中光高效耦合的关键因素。

Method: 利用布儒斯特异常退局域化（Brewster-anomaly delocalization）机制——即布儒斯特效应阻止多重散射干涉从而抵消局域化——与自由电子辐射的非常规协同作用，在强无序、非工程化的多层结构中实现自由电子辐射。

Result: 实现了在强无序多层介质中无局域化、高强度且定向的自由电子辐射；该辐射对随机介质构型、光频率和电子速度均表现出显著的不变性；为在复杂介质中实现此前难以企及频率的粒子探测器和消色差光源开辟了新机遇。

Conclusion: 布儒斯特异常退局域化可有效克服随机介质中自由电子-光耦合的局域化限制，实现构型不变、频率不变和速度不变的定向自由电子辐射，为易制备复杂介质中的粒子探测和消色差光源应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Localization effects are central to disordered electronics and photonics. In electronics, Anderson localization governs electron confinement in randomly perturbed lattices. Similarly, its photonic counterpart inhibits light transport via disorder -- but with a unique exception: Brewster-anomaly delocalization, where the Brewster effect prevents multiple-scattering interference and counteracts the localization. Despite extensive research in electronics and photonics separately, the intricate role of localization effects in free-electron--light interactions -- vital for lasers, accelerators, microscopy and spectroscopy, and quantum information -- remains largely unexplored. At the same time, localization effects are widely regarded as a key factor limiting the efficient coupling between free electrons and light in random media. Here we overcome this key limitation via the unconventional interplay between Brewster-anomaly delocalization and free-electron radiation. In this way, free-electron radiation can be localization-free, intense and directional even in strongly disordered, unengineered multilayers. Essentially, this delocalization-mediated free-electron radiation is remarkably invariant not only to the random-medium configuration, but also to the light frequency and the electron velocity. Our findings unlock new opportunities for particle detectors and achromatic light sources operating in easy-to-fabricate complex media at previously inaccessible frequencies.

</details>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [A Periodically Interacting Dark Sector: Signatures and Constraints from CMB and Cosmic Expansion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26174)
*Marco Antonio Cardoso Alvarez,Micol Benetti,Leila Graef,Robert Brandenberger*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 提出一种新宇宙学模型，有效描述超哈勃涨落对宇宙背景的反作用，现象学上表现为暗区能量传递符号周期性变化，通过CMB、BAO、Ia型超新星数据约束，发现数据允许显著振幅的振荡相互作用，但无统计上优于ΛCDM的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超哈勃尺度涨落对宇宙背景的反作用（back-reaction）是任何宇宙学场景中预期会出现的普适效应，但目前缺乏有效的唯象描述框架。本文旨在构建一个无需额外成分即可刻画这一效应的模型。

Method: 构建包含超哈勃涨落反作用有效描述的宇宙学模型，该模型在现象学层面可映射为相互作用暗区模型，其能量传递符号随时间周期性改变。利用宇宙微波背景辐射（CMB）、重子声学振荡（BAO）和Ia型超新星观测数据对模型参数进行联合约束。

Result: 数据分析表明，该模型允许存在显著振幅的振荡型相互作用项；然而，与标准ΛCDM模型相比，并未呈现出统计上显著的偏好。

Conclusion: 该模型为超哈勃涨落反作用提供了一种可行的有效描述，且在当前观测精度下与ΛCDM兼容。未来更精确的数据或可进一步检验此类振荡相互作用信号。

Abstract: We introduce a novel cosmological model that provides an effective description of the back-reaction of super-Hubble fluctuations on the cosmological background, a generic effect expected to arise in any cosmological scenario without requiring additional ingredients. At the phenomenological level, it can be interpreted as an interacting dark sector scenario in which the sign of the energy transfer changes periodically over time. We constrain the model using CMB, BAO, and Type Ia supernova data. We find that a significant amplitude of the oscillatory interaction is allowed by the data, although there is no statistically significant preference for this model over $Λ$CDM.

</details>


### [105] [Domain walls through different cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28214)
*I. Dankovsky,D. Gorbunov,S. Ramazanov,A. Vikman*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过格点模拟研究了不同早期宇宙宇宙学（从尘埃主导到硬物质主导乃至有效闵可夫斯基空间）中双阱势模型畴壁网络的演化，发现畴壁网络演化对宇宙状态方程具有近似普适性，粒子视界而非哈勃率是决定网络演化的关键因素，但在封闭畴壁形成和引力波谱特征方面存在普适性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究早期宇宙不同的宇宙学状态方程（EoS）对畴壁网络演化的影响，确定控制畴壁网络现象学（尤其是引力波发射）的关键物理量究竟是哈勃率还是粒子视界，并检验畴壁网络演化是否存在普适性。

Method: 采用格点模拟（lattice simulations），在双阱势模型中模拟畴壁网络演化，覆盖从尘埃主导、辐射主导、硬物质主导到有效闵可夫斯基空间极限的多种宇宙学背景，并分析畴壁面积密度、关联长度及引力波谱等观测量对EoS的依赖性。

Result: 1）畴壁面积主要由粒子视界决定，宇宙膨胀细节起次要作用，网络演化呈现近似普适性；2）封闭畴壁的形成对EoS高度敏感，违反普适性，但其对总网络贡献太小，不足以改变整体图景；3）有偏畴壁湮灭产生的引力波谱中，红外斜率依赖于EoS参数，紫外部分出现平台且随EoS变硬而愈加显著，但在最受关注的近峰区域，EoS依赖性较弱；4）给出了多种原始宇宙学背景下谱峰附近区域的拟合公式。

Conclusion: 畴壁网络演化以粒子视界为关键控制量，对宇宙状态方程具有近似普适性；引力波谱在近峰区域对EoS依赖较弱，有利于脉冲星计时阵列等引力波探测的模型预言；但封闭畴壁形成和谱的红外/紫外特征仍存在普适性破缺。

Abstract: We study properties of domain walls (DWs) arising in the model with a double well potential assuming different early universe cosmologies: from dust through stiff matter domination to the limit of effective Minkowski space. Using lattice simulations we demonstrate that evolution of DW networks exhibits an approximate universality with respect to the cosmological equation of state (EoS). Namely, the wall area inside a given large volume is mainly determined by the particle horizon, while details of cosmic expansion play a subdominant role. As it follows, particle horizon rather than the inverse Hubble rate is pivotal in DW evolution defining the network correlation length and hence its phenomenology, e.g., the characteristic wavelength of emitted gravitational waves (GWs). Formation of closed DWs is shown to be very sensitive to the cosmological EoS and hence violate the universality, but their contribution to the total network is too small to change the overall picture. The universality breaking is also observed in the spectral properties of GWs from annihilating (biased) DWs: i) the IR slope of the spectrum depends on the EoS parameter; ii) there is a plateau in the UV part of the spectrum, which gets more pronounced as one stiffens the EoS. However, the dependence on the EoS is rather weak in the near peak region, which is most relevant from the viewpoint of pulsar timing arrays and other searches for the stochastic GW background. For a selection of primordial cosmologies, we provide fitting formulae for the spectra near the peak.

</details>


### [106] [GGI Lectures on Large-Scale Structure Perturbation Theory (Effective Field Theory)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28314)
*Mikhail M. Ivanov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文是一份面向本科生和初入研究生阶段学生的讲义，系统介绍了用于宇宙大尺度结构的非线性微扰理论，涵盖有效场论框架、标准微扰理论的缺陷、重子声学振荡的非线性演化、圈图重整化、星系偏袒与红移空间畸变的EFT处理，以及拉格朗日微扰理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为本科生和初级研究生提供一套从零基础出发、无需宇宙学或量子场论先修知识的完整教学框架，以理解并建模观测到的星系成团现象（包括实空间和红移空间），填补现有教材在有效场论方法应用于大尺度结构方面的教学空白。

Method: 基于牛顿宇宙学框架，利用对称性原理构建大尺度结构有效场论；系统分析标准微扰理论的不足；讨论重子声学振荡非线性演化与等效原理的关系；引入抵消项和圈图重整化处理；发展星系偏袒和红移空间畸变的EFT描述；最后介绍拉格朗日微扰理论。

Result: 该讲义展示了EFT方法如何系统性地解决标准微扰理论的缺陷，通过引入抵消项和随机效应成功重整化圈图发散，并自然地描述星系偏袒、红移空间畸变及红移空间随机性等观测效应，为当前和未来星系巡天提供了坚实的理论建模工具。

Conclusion: 本文以自洽的教学方式，从对称性和有效场论的基本思想出发，完整构建了大尺度结构非线性微扰理论体系，强调了EFT在克服标准微扰理论局限、精确描述星系成团统计性质方面的核心作用，对理解巡天数据背后的宇宙学信息具有重要意义。

Abstract: These notes are an introduction to non-linear perturbation theory for cosmological large-scale structure. They are aimed at undergraduate and beginning graduate students and do not require any cosmology or quantum field theory background. All necessary concepts are developed from scratch. The lectures are intended to explain all key ingredients needed to model the observed clustering of galaxies in real and redshift spaces. After a brief pedagogical introduction to the ideas of effective field theory (EFT), we develop large-scale structure EFT in the context of Newtonian cosmology using symmetry principles. We discuss in detail the shortcomings of Standard Perturbation Theory, the non-linear evolution of baryon acoustic oscillations and its relation to the equivalence principle, counterterms and renormalization of the loop diagrams, and stochastic effects. Then we develop EFT for galaxy bias and redshift space distortions. We also highlight some important facts about redshift-space stochasticity, relevant for ongoing and future galaxy surveys. Finally, we introduce Lagrangian Perturbation Theory.

</details>


### [107] [Inflation, Open Universes, and Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28445)
*Anton Chudaykin,Mikhail M. Ivanov,Renata Kallosh,Andrei Linde,Oliver H. E. Philcox,Yusuke Yamada*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了空间曲率（Ω_k）和动力学暗能量（w_0, w_a）对原初标量谱指数 n_s 的影响。结果表明，引入微小的负曲率（Ω_k ≈ 3×10⁻³）或时变暗能量可降低 n_s 值，使其更接近 Starobinsky、Higgs 及最简 α-attractor 暴胀模型的预言，从而缓解 ΛCDM 框架下观测数据与这些暴胀模型之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准 ΛCDM 模型下，CMB 观测得到的谱指数 n_s 与 Starobinsky、Higgs 及最简 α-attractor 暴胀模型的预言之间存在张力。作者旨在探究这种张力是否可以通过扩展宇宙学模型——即引入空间曲率或动力学暗能量——来缓解或消除。

Method: 联合分析 CMB 数据集（Planck、SPT、ACT）与 DESI 光谱星系样本（包含 BAO 和全形状成团性测量），在 ΛCDM 基础上扩展参数空间，分别引入空间曲率参数 Ω_k 以及动力学暗能量参数（w_0, w_a），通过 MCMC 等方法约束 n_s 及其他宇宙学参数。

Result: 1. 引入 Ω_k ≈ -0.003 的微小负曲率后，n_s 降至 0.9667±0.0041（Planck+DESI）或 0.9692±0.0035（加入 ACT+SPT）；2. 允许时变暗能量同样使 n_s 降低，联合数据给出 n_s=0.9716±0.0032，叠加负曲率后降至 0.9694±0.0035；3. 上述结果均显示出与 Starobinsky、Higgs 及最简 α-attractor 模型预言的更好一致性，表明张力仅在 ΛCDM 框架下成立。

Conclusion: 观测与 Starobinsky/Higgs/α-attractor 暴胀模型之间的 n_s 张力并非本质性的，可通过扩展背景宇宙学模型（开放宇宙或动力学暗能量）得以缓解。文章还讨论了这些发现对开放宇宙及动力学暗能量背景下暴胀模型的启示，并指出未来 DESI-II、SPHEREx、Euclid、Rubin、Roman 等数据将进一步检验相关模型。

Abstract: We study the impact of spatial curvature ($Ω_k$) and dynamical dark energy (parametrized by $w_0$ and $w_a$) on the spectral index $n_s$ using a combination of cosmic microwave background datasets (Planck, SPT, and ACT), and spectroscopic galaxy samples from DESI, including both BAO and full-shape clustering measurements. We show that a small negative curvature, $Ω_k\simeq 3\times 10^{-3}$, lowers the value of $n_s$, bringing it closer to predictions of the Starobinsky, Higgs, and simplest $α$-attractor inflationary models. In particular, we find $n_s= 0.9667\pm0.0041$ (using Planck and DESI data) or $n_s= 0.9692\pm0.0035$ (adding ACT and SPT). Allowing for time-evolving dark energy also reduces the spectral index, leading to $n_s=0.9716\pm0.0032$ (from the combined dataset), or $n_s=0.9694\pm0.0035$ in combination with a small negative curvature. Our results demonstrate that the tension between current observational data and the Starobinsky, Higgs, and simplest $α$-attractor models holds only for $Λ$CDM, and can be mitigated in extended cosmological models. We discuss implications of these findings for inflationary models in an open universe and/or with dynamical dark energy, including scenarios with quantum tunneling and non-standard topology. Furthermore, we briefly describe a special class of $α$-attractor models, where one can make $n_s$ arbitrarily large, and we describe the $α$-attractor quintessence model. Such models may be of particular relevance when future data from DESI, as well as DESI-II, SPHEREx, Euclid, Rubin, and Roman, becomes available.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond monomial $α$-attractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28502)
*Laura Iacconi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了超越单项式α-吸引子T模型的可能性，具体分析了一个由二次项和四次项组成的二项式势。研究发现，当四次项系数满足0 < c+1/2 ≪ 1时，标量谱指数n_s可达0.965，且对α的依赖性与单项式势存在质的差异。此外，在再加热阶段，四次项可能先主导，导致状态方程从w̄ ~ 1/3演化到w̄ → 0。结果表明，单项式模型假设状态方程由单一幂次决定需要显著的精细调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期小尺度CMB数据与DESI数据结合后倾向于更大的标量谱指数n_s，而单项式α-吸引子T模型仅在双曲正切函数幂次p≥6（p为偶数）时才能与此观测一致。然而，p>2的超引力构造依赖于低次幂项在整个暴胀和再加热过程中可忽略的假设。本文旨在探索超越单项式形式的后果，检验这一假设的合理性。

Method: 作者考虑了一个二项式势（二次项+四次项），分析其在暴胀阶段对n_s和α依赖性的影响。通过求解再加热最初几个e-fold的背景动力学方程，追踪暴胀子振荡幅度衰减过程中二次项与四次项的交替主导行为，进而推导出随时间演化的状态方程w̄。

Result: 1）二项式模型产生非普适的n_s行为，最大值可达0.965，且其α依赖性与单项式势定性不同；2）再加热阶段四次项可先主导，状态方程初始为w̄ ~ 1/3，最终趋于w̄ → 0；3）要实现约4个e-fold的四次项主导再加热阶段，需要四次项与二次项系数之比c ~ 10⁵（当α ≳ 0.1时），即需要显著的参数层级。

Conclusion: 超越单项式形式的模型可导致n_s偏离单项式预言的显著非平庸偏差。此外，再加热过程中状态方程由单一p次幂唯一决定的假设依赖于超引力势的实质精细调节，这对大p值单项式模型的使用提出了质疑。

Abstract: Recent small-scale CMB data show a preference for larger scalar spectral index, $n_s$, when combined with DESI data. Monomial $α$-attractor T-models can be reconciled with the new observations if the power of the hyperbolic tangent function is $p\geq 6$, where $p$ is even. The supergravity construction of monomial T-models with $p>2$ relies on the assumption that lower powers remain negligible over the whole field range explored during inflation and reheating. What would be the consequences of going beyond the monomial formulation? As a first step in this direction, we consider the case of a binomial potential, given by the sum of quadratic and quartic terms. When the quartic coefficient, $c$, is $0< c+1/2 \ll 1$, we find that the new model displays non-universal behavior for $n_s$, leading to values as large as $0.965$ and with an $α$-dependence that is qualitatively different from that of monomial potentials. By solving the background dynamics during the first few e-folds of perturbative reheating we show that the quartic term might dominate before the quadratic one eventually takes over as the inflaton oscillations decrease in amplitude. This leads to a time-dependent equation of state, with $\bar w\sim 1/3$ initially before ultimately $\bar w\to 0$. Obtaining a quartic-dominated reheating stage lasting $\sim4$ e-folds requires a substantial hierarchy between the quartic and quadratic terms, $c\sim 10^5$ for $α\gtrsim 0.1$. Our study highlights that models beyond the monomial form can lead to non-trivial deviations of $n_s$ from the predictions of monomial potentials. Furthermore our results for $\bar w$ during reheating call into question the use of monomial models with large $p$; assuming that $\bar w$ is uniquely determined by the $p$-th power relies on substantial fine-tuning of the underlying supergravity potential.

</details>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Third-order nonlinear transport in a percolative two-dimensional superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27641)
*Wenjun Liu,Chenghe Wang,Xiubin Li,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Tao Zhang,Xiao-Xiao Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在双层范德华超导体1T'-MoTe₂的渗透转变区观测到显著的三阶非线性输运，其三次谐波电压与激励电流呈立方关系，可由含时Ginzburg-Landau理论中的超导涨落半定量解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德华材料中因降维、量子涨落增强及复杂电声耦合常出现渗透超导现象，其中正常电子与库珀对共存。然而，该体系中高阶非线性输运行为尚未得到充分探索，亟需新的灵敏探针来表征此类渗透超导转变。

Method: 在三层1T'-MoTe₂超导体的渗透转变温区，测量三次谐波纵向电压（V∥³ω）随激励电流的变化，分析其非线性系数与超导态的关联，并利用含时Ginzburg-Landau理论中的超导涨落模型对实验结果进行半定量拟合。

Result: 观测到V∥³ω在阈值以下对激励电流呈现明显的立方依赖关系，其幅值和非线性系数均与超导态强烈耦合。该非线性响应可由涨落库珀对在含时Ginzburg-Landau框架下较好地半定量描述。

Conclusion: 三阶非线性输运可作为渗透超导体系中超导转变的灵敏探针，并为强关联系统中探索更高阶输运现象奠定了基础。

Abstract: Percolative superconductivity frequently arises in two-dimensional van der Waals materials due to reduced dimensionality, enhanced quantum fluctuations, and complex electron-phonon interactions, providing a unique platform where normal electrons coexist with Cooper pairs. We report the observation of substantial third-order nonlinear transport in a trilayer $1T^\prime$-MoTe$_2$ superconductor within its percolative transition regime. The third-harmonic longitudinal voltage ($V_{\|}^{3ω}$) exhibits a clear cubic dependence on excitation current below a threshold, with both its magnitude and nonlinear coefficient strongly correlated with the superconducting state. This nonlinear response is semiquantitatively captured by the superconducting fluctuation within the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau theory, where nonlinear transport arises due to fluctuating Cooper pairs. Our results demonstrate that third-order nonlinear transport serves as a sensitive probe of superconducting transitions in percolative systems and establish a foundation for exploring higher-order transport phenomena in strongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [110] [Anomalous metal and superconducting phases in rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28425)
*Anna Okounkova,Abigail Sohm,Tobias Faehndrich,Manish Kumar,Derek Waleffe,Jiaqiang Yan,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Joshua Folk,Matthew Yankowitz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在菱方石墨烯/WSe₂体系中，作者系统研究了超导态中电阻无法降至零的反常行为，发现栅压空间中存在零电阻超导与有限饱和电阻区域共存的现象。有限电阻态再现了"反常金属"的关键特征，该体系的洁净度和可调控性为理解这一长期悬而未决的现象提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在石墨烯基二维超导体系中，许多栅压调控的超导相虽展现出非常规配对证据，但电阻在降温时并不完全消失，而是饱和在一个有限值。这一"反常金属"态在薄膜超导体中已被观测数十年，却缺乏公认的理论解释。作者旨在利用菱方石墨烯体系对该行为进行系统研究，以排除外在因素、厘清其本质。

Method: 作者采用WSe₂衬底上的菱方石墨烯器件，在栅压空间中系统扫描，测量电阻随温度、垂直磁场、面内磁场和电流的变化，对比零电阻超导区域与有限饱和电阻区域的相变行为。

Result: 零场下，零电阻超导与有限饱和电阻区域在栅压空间中呈孤立口袋状分布，但两者在温度、垂直磁场和电流超过临界值时均表现出向正常态的突变。施加小面内磁场后，这些口袋扩展并融合，在毫开尔文基温下形成零电阻态与有限电阻态之间的尖锐边界。有限电阻态完整复现了反常金属的核心现象学特征。

Conclusion: 菱方石墨烯体系的超高洁净度和栅压可调性排除了许多外在解释（如不均匀性或耗散），为反常金属态提供了严格的实验约束和理想的研究平台，有助于推动这一长期谜题的理论突破。

Abstract: Two-dimensional superconductivity is now well established in graphene-based systems, with many such realizations showing evidence for unconventional pairing. Yet in several of the gate-tuned phases that otherwise exhibit clear signatures of superconductivity, the resistance does not vanish as temperature is lowered, instead saturating at a finite value. Here we report a systematic study of this behavior in rhombohedral graphene on a WSe$_2$ substrate, finding regions of gate space with zero-resistance superconductivity alongside others with finite saturation resistance. At zero magnetic field, these regions appear as isolated pockets in gate space that otherwise exhibit strikingly similar phenomenology, including abrupt transitions to the normal state as temperature, perpendicular magnetic field, and current are raised above critical values. A small in-plane field expands and merges these pockets without qualitatively altering their behavior, producing a sharp boundary at millikelvin base temperature between states of zero or finite resistance. The finite-resistance state reproduces key phenomenology associated with the anomalous metal, a state that has been observed in thin-film superconductors for decades but lacks an accepted theoretical explanation. The tunability and reproducibility of ultra-clean rhombohedral graphene place strong constraints on extrinsic explanations and provide a new platform for understanding this behavior.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [SU(3)-structures on quotients of 3-Sasakian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28337)
*Quentin Peres,Dimitrios Tsimpis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了一个7维拟正则3-Sasakian轨形关于一个Reeb向量场的商空间继承了一个由两个实参数刻画的SU(3)-结构，计算了其挠率并证明其为LT-结构；特定参数值下可得到近Kähler结构，并给出了多个实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究3-Sasakian几何与SU(3)-结构之间的联系，特别是通过Reeb商构造从7维3-Sasakian轨形得到带有特殊几何结构的6维流形或轨形，并揭示这些结构的挠率分类与性质。

Method: 作者从一个7维拟正则3-Sasakian轨形出发，对其一个Reeb向量场作商映射，得到6维商空间Z。在Z上构造了一族由两个实参数(a,b)刻画的SU(3)-结构，详细计算了该结构的本征挠率类，验证其满足LT-结构（Lee形式与挠率满足特定关系）的条件；进一步分析参数特化情形，证明适当选取参数可退化为近Kähler结构。最后在正则、拟正则及轨形三种情形下给出了具体例子。

Result: 商空间Z上的SU(3)-结构确实为LT-结构；当参数满足特定条件时，该结构成为近Kähler结构。论文在正则情形、拟正则情形以及轨形情形下均构造了相应的例证。

Conclusion: 7维拟正则3-Sasakian轨形的Reeb商自然地携带一参数化的SU(3)-结构，且该结构属于LT-类型，这为研究3-Sasakian几何与SU(3)-结构理论之间的桥梁提供了新视角；同时近Kähler结构作为特例出现，表明该构造具有一定的几何丰富性。

Abstract: We show that if $(S,g,ξ_i,η_i,Φ_i)$ is a quasi-regular $3$-Sasakian orbifold of dimension $7$, then its quotient $Z$ by one of the Reeb vector fields inherits an $SU(3)$-structure parametrized by two real parameters. We compute its torsion and show that it is an LT-structure. Moreover, for certain values of the parameters, we can provide a nearly Kähler structure on $Z$ appearing as a special case of our construction. We give several examples in the regular, quasi-regular and orbifold cases.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Adaptive Nyström for Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27427)
*Lulu Kang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应Nyström近似方法，通过贪心选择地标点以最小化核近似误差的迹残差，并将地标扩展与超参数优化交替进行。该方法在精度和稳定性上显著优于随机选择，同时保持线性扩展，可达到与精确GP推断相当的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程回归(GPR)的O(n³)计算复杂度限制了其在大规模数据上的可扩展性。低秩Nyström近似虽能将复杂度降至O(nm²)，但其精度严重依赖地标点的选择质量，而现有静态选择方法无法在协方差结构优化过程中动态调整。

Method: 提出自适应Nyström方法：(1)采用贪心策略逐一选择地标点，每一步以最小化核近似误差的迹残差(trace residual)为准则；(2)将地标点扩展与超参数优化交替进行，使地标选择能随着协方差结构精化而自适应调整。

Result: 在五个基准函数上的数值实验表明：该方法在精度和稳定性上均显著优于随机地标选择；预测性能与精确GP推断相当，同时计算复杂度随样本量呈线性扩展。

Conclusion: 该方法为大规模计算机实验提供了一种原则性强、高效且可线性扩展的GPR框架，成功解决了Nyström近似中地标点选择的核心难题。

Abstract: Gaussian Process Regression (GPR) is a robust framework for uncertainty quantification, yet its $O(n^3)$ complexity limits its scalability. Low-rank Nyström approximations can reduce this burden to $O(nm^2)$, but their accuracy depends heavily on the selection of landmark points. We propose an adaptive Nyström approach that greedily selects landmarks to minimize the trace residual of the kernel approximation error. Unlike static approximations, our method interleaves landmark expansion with hyperparameter optimization, allowing the selection process to adapt as the covariance structure is refined. Numerical experiments on five benchmark functions demonstrate that this method significantly outperforms random landmark selection in both accuracy and stability. It achieves predictive performance comparable to exact GP inference while maintaining linear scaling with respect to the sample size, providing a principled and efficient framework for large-scale computer experiments.

</details>


### [113] [A Mathematical Framework for Topological Causal Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28161)
*Hugo Gobato Souto,Ioannis Diamantis*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑因果数据分析(TCDA)框架，将观测空间、因果模型类、拓扑表示和因果查询分离，区分结果层面和分布层面的拓扑分析，建立Banach空间值摘要的识别与双重稳健表示，并通过g-公式实现分布层面的目标识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据（图像、点云、网络、空间场等）作为结构化对象，其个体处理效应Y¹-Y⁰可能无定义或在科学上不充分。需要一种框架，在因果假设确定后，利用拓扑提供稳定且对形状敏感的摘要，而非用拓扑来定义干预。

Method: 1. 建立TCDA框架，明确分离四个层面：观测空间、因果模型类、拓扑表示、因果查询。2. 区分结果层面TCDA（变换个体潜在结果）和分布层面TCDA（变换干预结果分布律），刻画两者对比一致的条件。3. 基于已有结果层面理论，建立Banach空间值摘要的识别与双重稳健表示。4. 在分布层面通过标准因果g-公式进行目标识别，推导稳定性迁移界和插件一致性。5. 提出目标特定拓扑可忽略性概念，阐明何时可在不识别完整干预律的情况下识别协变量标准化的粗效应。6. 界定观测拓扑在因果发现中的作用。

Result: 1. 建立了统一的TCDA框架。2. 得到Banach空间值摘要的识别和双重稳健表示。3. 通过g-公式实现分布层面目标识别，并推导出稳定性迁移界和插件一致性。4. 确立了目标特定拓扑可忽略性的条件。5. 证明观测拓扑可辅助受限模型类上的诊断，但本身不能识别因果结构。

Conclusion: TCDA框架成功将拓扑方法与因果推断结合，拓扑不定义干预而是提供形状敏感的稳定摘要。该框架明确了不同层面分析的一致条件，建立了严谨的识别理论，并严格限定了拓扑在因果发现中的辅助性角色——它不能独立识别因果结构。

Abstract: Many modern outcomes, including images, point clouds, networks, and spatial fields, are structured objects for which \(Y^1-Y^0\) may be undefined or scientifically inadequate. We introduce \emph{Topological Causal Data Analysis} (TCDA), a framework separating the observation space, causal-model class, topological representation, and causal query. Topology does not define interventions; it supplies stable, shape-sensitive summaries after causal assumptions have been specified. We distinguish outcome-level TCDA, which transforms individual potential outcomes, from distribution-level TCDA, which transforms interventional outcome laws, and characterize when outcome and distribution level contrasts agree. Building on recent outcome-level theory, we formulate identification and doubly robust representations for Banach-space-valued summaries. At the distribution level, we identify targets through the standard causal \(g\)-formula and derive stability-transfer bounds and plug-in consistency. We also place target-specific topological ignorability within the framework, clarifying when a covariate-standardized coarse effect can be identified without identifying the full interventional laws. Finally, we delimit the role of observational topology in causal discovery: it can assist diagnosis on restricted model classes but cannot by itself identify causal structure.

</details>


### [114] [Non-partitioned e-detectors for nonparametric sequential change detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28322)
*Aytijhya Saha,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究在完全未知分布族下的序贯变点检测问题，提出基于点零假设e-process聚合的通用检测框架，通过取候选无变化分布的下确界，实现对平均运行长度(ARL)和虚警概率(PFA)的控制，并在一阶渐近意义下达到最优检测延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的序贯变点检测方法通常需要预先指定变化前和变化后的分布族，且假定两者之间存在已知的划分。然而，在实际应用中，这种先验划分往往不可得，且分布族可能非常一般。因此，需要一种无需预划分、能处理更广泛分布类的通用检测方法。

Method: 作者提出一种通用的序贯变点检测器类。核心思路是对所有可能变点位置上的点零假设e-process进行加权聚合，并取所有候选无变化分布的下确界。权重聚合方案决定了检测器是达到ARL控制还是PFA控制。该方法不依赖对分布族的预先划分，适用范围涵盖一般概率分布类$\mathcal P$。

Result: 在适当假设下，证明所提方法达到一阶渐近最优的检测延迟。具体应用实例包括：亚高斯和有界均值变化、未知方差的高斯均值变化、以及马尔可夫转移矩阵的变化检测。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于e-process聚合的框架为完全未知分布下的序贯变点检测提供了统一且理论完备的解决方案，既保证了运行长度或虚警概率的严格控制，又在渐近意义上达到最优检测效率，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We study the problem of sequential change detection over a general class of probability distributions ($\mathcal P$), where both the pre-change and post-change distributions are unknown and belong to $\mathcal P$. We do not assume a pre-specified partition of $\mathcal P$ into pre- and post-change families. We propose a general class of sequential change detectors obtained by aggregating point-null e-processes over possible changepoints and taking an infimum over candidate no-change distributions. The weights in the aggregation scheme determine whether they attain average run length (ARL) control and probability-of-false-alarm (PFA) control. Under suitable assumptions, we prove that our methods achieve first-order asymptotically optimal detection delay. Concrete examples include sub-Gaussian and bounded mean changes, Gaussian mean changes with unknown variance, as well as changes in Markov transition matrices.

</details>

### [115] [Three-Photon Bayesian Imaging of Ortho-Positronium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27741)
*L. Raczynski,W. Krzemien,A. Coussat,M. Bala,B. C. Hiesmayr,K. Klimaszewski,M. Obara,R. Y. Shopa*

Main category: physics.med-ph

TL;DR: 提出TRIO算法，在贝叶斯最大后验概率框架下统一了基于时间和能量的三光子符合事件逐个重建，首次引入基于QED的物理先验，将定位误差降至1.62 cm，并兼容标准核素与现有TOF-PET扫描仪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正电子湮灭中约40%经Ps形成，其中o-Ps部分衰变为三光子，携带微环境信息（如三光子/两光子产额比）可作为诊断标志物。现有正电子素寿命成像依赖瞬发光子，受限于特定核素；纯能量法精度差（~18 cm），纯时间法精度有限（~3 cm），亟需一种能兼容标准核素的高精度三光子重建方法。

Method: 提出TRIO算法：将三光子事件逐个重建建模为贝叶斯最大后验推断问题，在单一概率框架内统一了(1)基于时间的三角定位（trilateration）、(2)基于能量的重建、以及(3)首次引入源自QED对Ps衰变描述的物理先验。该算法仅依赖三个光子，无需瞬发光子，兼容18F等标准核素。

Result: 基于Siemens Biograph Quadra扫描仪的蒙特卡罗模拟：TRIO平均定位误差1.62 cm，比纯时间三角定位（3.05 cm）提升约2倍，比纯能量重建（18 cm）提升约一个数量级。

Conclusion: TRIO算法显著提升了三光子湮灭事件的定位精度，且兼容现有可记录三光子符合事件的TOF-PET扫描仪，为基于三光子/两光子产额比的微环境诊断提供了可行的图像重建方案。

Abstract: PET provides functional images relying on two-photon coincidences from positron-electron annihilation. In human tissue, about 40\% of annihilations are preceded by Ps formation, of which o-Ps component partially decays into three photons, with the remainder annihilating via pick-off or spin-exchange into two photons. This three-photon channel carries additional information about the surrounding micro-environment, including the three-to-two-photon yield ratio as a potential diagnostic marker. We propose the TRIO algorithm, a novel three-photon event-by-event image reconstruction algorithm formulated as a Bayesian maximum a posteriori inference problem. TRIO unifies time-based trilateration, energy-based reconstruction and, for the first time, a physics-informed prior derived from the QED description of Ps decay within a single probabilistic framework.
  In contrast to positronium lifetime imaging, which requires a prompt photon and is therefore restricted to specific radionuclides, TRIO relies solely on the three photons and is fully compatible with standard radionuclides such as 18F.
  Monte Carlo simulation modelled after the Siemens Biograph Quadra scanner demonstrates a mean position error of 1.62~cm, improving by approximately a factor of two over the time-based trilateration (3.05 cm) and by about an order of magnitude over energy-based reconstruction alone (18 cm). More importantly, the proposed Bayesian approach is compatible with existing TOF-PET scanners that can register three-photon annihilation coincidences.

</details>

### [116] [Extended s-wave superconductivity in M-point twisted bilayer SnSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27492)
*Lennart Klebl,Ammon Fischer,Salahudin V. Smailagić,Ming-Rui Li,Henning Schlömer,Haoyu Hu,B. Andrei Bernevig,Dante M. Kennes,Ronny Thomale*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理Wannier模型和泛函重整化群方法，研究了AB堆叠扭转SnSe₂的M谷摩尔纹材料中的电子序和非常规超导电性，揭示了掺杂驱动下从反铁磁序到谷选择自旋涨落介导的超导态的转变，并发现其与铁基超导体具有现象学上的相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索M谷（M-valley）摩尔纹材料中电子序和非常规超导电性的涌现机制。该类材料的平带位于布里渊区M点，与常见的K谷摩尔纹体系不同，其电子关联和费米面不稳定性尚待系统研究。

Method: （1）从AB堆叠扭转SnSe₂的第一性原理出发构建Wannier模型；（2）采用泛函重整化群（fRG）模拟，解析动量结构和能量尺度，处理门电压屏蔽的长程库仑相互作用；（3）系统分析不同掺杂浓度下半填充反铁磁条纹态的费米面不稳定性。

Result: 半填充（ν=3）时体系处于反铁磁条纹态；大剂量空穴掺杂（ν≈1）导致弱耦合超导体，呈现多种配对对称性；轻微电子和空穴掺杂（ν≈2,4）则稳定了自旋单态的扩展s波超导态，其配对受益于偏离费米能级的虚粒子-空穴态间散射。

Conclusion: M点摩尔纹材料可作为一种量子模拟平台，其物理性质与铁基磷族超导体具有显著的现象学对应关系，为理解非常规超导机制提供了新的研究途径。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of electronic order and unconventional superconductivity in M-valley moiré materials. Starting from a first-principles Wannier model of AB-stacked twisted SnSe2, we tackle the (gate-screened) long-ranged Coulomb interaction with functional renormalization group simulations resolving the momentum structure and energy scales of the leading Fermi surface instabilities. Upon doping an antiferromagnetic stripe state at half-filling ($ν=3$ electrons per moiré unit cell) of the moiré flat bands, magnetic order gives way to unconventional superconductivity mediated by valley-selective spin fluctuations: Large hole doping ($ν\approx1$) leads to weak-coupling superconductors with various pairing symmetries, while slight electron- and hole-doping ($ν\approx2,4$) stabilizes a spin-singlet, extended s-wave state that benefits from scattering between virtual particle and hole states that are detuned from the Fermi level. These findings establish M-point moiré materials as a quantum simulation platform with phenomenological parallels to the class of iron pnictide superconductors.

</details>


### [117] [Single-crystal structural phase diagram of stoichiometric bilayer nickelate La3Ni2O7 under hydrostatic pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27607)
*Misaki Sasaki,Zhehong Liu,Takeshi Hara,Shunsuke Kitou,Markus Kriener,Haruto Yoshimochi,Shion Yamada,Chieko Terakura,Naohisa Hirao,Hirokazu Kadobayashi,Yusuke Wakabayashi,Yoshinori Tokura,Yasujiro Taguchi,Taka-hisa Arima,Yukako Fujishiro*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过氦气作为传压介质的静水压实验，建立了化学计量比La3Ni2O7单晶的完整压力-温度相图，确定了超导电性出现在约10 GPa时由极性正交Am2m相（具有电荷序和NiO6八面体扭转）向四方I4/mmm相转变之后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍氧化物La3Ni2O7在压力下发现高温超导后，其超导相的晶体结构一直存在争议，这阻碍了对超导微观机理的理解。需要厘清超导区间的本征结构演化。

Method: 采用氦气作为传压介质以实现静水压条件，对化学计量比La3Ni2O7单晶进行高压X射线衍射及物性测量，建立了压力-温度相图。

Result: 常压下La3Ni2O7为极性正交Am2m结构，特征为不等价Ni位间的电荷序及NiO6八面体扭转；约10 GPa时发生从电荷有序Am2m相到四方I4/mmm相的一级结构相变，该相变与体超导电性的起始压力重合。

Conclusion: 确立了La3Ni2O7的本征结构演化路径，超导电性出现在四方I4/mmm相中，为镍酸盐超导的微观理论提供了明确的结构框架。

Abstract: The bilayer nickelate La3Ni2O7 has attracted intense interest following the discovery of high-temperature superconductivity under pressure, representing the first nickelate superconductor realized in bulk form. However, the crystal structure of the superconducting phase remains under active discussion, complicating efforts to establish its microscopic origin. Here we resolve these structural controversies by establishing a definitive pressure-temperature phase diagram, including the superconducting region of stoichiometric La3Ni2O7 single crystals under hydrostatic conditions using helium as the pressure-transmitting medium. At ambient pressure, La3Ni2O7 adopts a polar orthorhombic Am2m structure characterized by charge order between inequivalent Ni sites and NiO6 octahedral tilting. Upon compression, the system undergoes a direct transition from the charge-ordered Am2m phase to the tetragonal I4/mmm phase near 10 GPa, coinciding with the onset of bulk superconductivity. These results establish the intrinsic structural evolution of La3Ni2O7 and provide a structural framework for microscopic theories of nickelate superconductivity.

</details>


### [118] [Anomalous Microwave Response in YBCO Resonators beyond the Two-Level-System Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27392)
*Kaiwen Zheng,Nathan J. Johnson,Nathan T. Thobaben,Sidharth Duthaluru,Haochen Shen,Denae T. Cherry,David S. Wisbey,Kater W. Murch*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了YBCO共面波导谐振器在毫开尔文至40 K温区的微波响应，发现其低温行为无法仅用传统双能级系统(TLS)模型解释，可能需引入顺磁响应等额外机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究YBCO薄膜CPW谐振器在极低温（毫开尔文）下的微波损耗和频率响应机制，以评估其在量子信息等低温微波应用中的潜力，并澄清其低温反常行为的物理起源。

Method: 在70 mK至40 K温区测量YBCO薄膜共面波导谐振器的内部品质因数(Qi)和谐振频率分数偏移(Δf/fr)，分析其温度依赖性和微波功率依赖性，并与标准TLS模型及顺磁响应模型进行对比。

Result: Qi在70 mK时为4×10³~10⁴，在约6 K处达到峰值8×10³~1.2×10⁴；低温下Qi和Δf/fr均随温度升高而增加，类似于TLS行为，但未在谐振频率所设温度尺度上饱和，且损耗无微波功率依赖性；频率上翘可用缺陷诱导局域磁矩或Andreev束缚态的顺磁响应更好描述，损耗呈近似对数温度依赖性。

Conclusion: YBCO谐振器的低温微波响应超出了传统TLS框架的解释范围，需考虑顺磁响应等机制。该工作确立了图形化YBCO谐振器的毫开尔文性能基准，并揭示了其低温物理的新颖复杂性。

Abstract: We report the microwave response of coplanar-waveguide (CPW) resonators fabricated from $\mathrm{YBa_2Cu_3O_{7-δ}}$ (YBCO) thin films over temperatures from approximately $70~\mathrm{mK}$ to $40~\mathrm{K}$. The resonators exhibit internal quality factors $Q_\mathrm{i}$ in the range of $4\times10^3$ to $10^4$ at 70 mK, which increase to a maximum of approximately $8\times10^3$ to $1.2\times10^4$ near $6~\mathrm{K}$. At low temperatures, both $Q_\mathrm{i}$ and the fractional shift of the resonance frequency $Δf_\mathrm{r}/f_\mathrm{r}$ increases with temperature, qualitatively resembling behavior commonly associated with two-level-system (TLS) defects. However, neither response saturates on the temperature scale set by the resonator frequency, and the loss exhibits no observable microwave-power dependence. We show that low-temperature frequency upturn may be better described by an additional paramagnetic response associated with defect-induced local moments or Andreev bound states, while the low-temperature loss follows an approximately logarithmic temperature dependence whose microscopic origin remains unresolved. These measurements establish the millikelvin performance of patterned YBCO resonators and show that their low-temperature response cannot be understood within the conventional TLS framework alone.

</details>

### [119] [Bose-Einstein condensation and superfluidity on a fuzzy sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27470)
*Vira Shyta,Flavio S. Nogueira,Ashley M. Cook*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对易（模糊）二维球面上的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）与超流性。发现非对易性通过引入额外尺度参数N，提升了有序相，使得BEC临界温度高于普通对易球面S²的情形。进一步，在超流响应分析中，研究发现模糊球面上的正常流体分数在热力学极限下呈现线性温度依赖（~T），而非普通二维系统的~T³行为，这一线性关系与铜氧化物高温超导体中的Uemura定律相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根据Hohenberg定理，二维空间中T>0时不存在BEC，但超流性可因伽利略不变性破缺而出现。作者希望探索在紧致二维非对易空间（模糊球面）上，非对易性如何影响BEC和超流性的行为，并揭示其与常规对易球面的本质差异。

Method: 将标量玻色场提升为N×N矩阵，N与非对易参数相关，引入额外尺度。分别分析模糊球面上理想气体与弱相互作用玻色气体中的BEC，计算临界温度并与对易球面对比。进而研究弱相互作用玻色系统的超流响应，将涡旋集体坐标推广到非对易球面。通过超流密度计算，比较模糊球面与对易球面在热力学极限下正常流体分数的温度依赖行为。

Result: （1）模糊球面上理想和弱相互作用玻色气体的BEC临界温度均高于对易S²球面。（2）涡旋集体坐标可在普通球面上诱导非对易性。（3）模糊球面的热力学极限与平面对易情形不同，在大球极限下正常流体分数呈线性~T依赖，而对易S²则呈现常规二维~T³行为。

Conclusion: 非对易性有利于有序相，增强BEC和超流性。模糊球面具有区别于平面和对易球面的独特热力学极限，其正常流体分数的线性温度依赖与铜氧化物高温超导体的Uemura定律类似，暗示非对易性可能在实验上具有重要关联。

Abstract: According to Hohenberg's theorem, Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) in two dimensions is impossible for any temperature $T>0$. By contrast, superfluidity does occur in two dimensions at finite temperatures; it emerges due to the breaking of Galilei invariance. Here we consider BEC and superfluidity on a compact two-dimensional space taking the form of a non-commutative ("fuzzy") sphere, where the scalar bosonic fields are promoted to $N\times N$ matrices. The dimension $N$ is related to the non-commutativity parameter of space and introduces an additional scale into the system. We find that non-commutativity favors ordered phases and so enhances BEC and superfluidity. We analyze BEC in ideal and weakly interacting Bose gases on a fuzzy sphere, finding in each case that the critical temperature of BEC is greater compared to that found in the case of a commutative sphere $S^2$. Then we investigate the superfluid response of weakly interacting Bose systems. To account for vortices in a superfluid, we show that, even on an ordinary sphere, the collective coordinates of vortices induce non-commutativity. With this in mind, we extend the definition of vortex defects to an inherently non-commutative sphere studied here, where the notion of a point is untenable. The non-commutativity is expected to be experimentally relevant to BEC and superfluidity since the fuzzy sphere has a thermodynamic limit distinct from the one defined over a plane, unlike the $S^2$ case. The significance of this difference is illustrated by the superfluid density calculation indicating that, in the large sphere limit, the normal fluid fraction on the fuzzy sphere yields a linear in $T$ dependence, while on a commutative $S^2$ it exhibits the usual two-dimensional $\sim T^3$ behavior. This linear dependence, arising directly from non-commutativity, is reminiscent of Uemura's law in cuprate high-$T_c$ superconductors.

</details>


### [120] [Observation of Moiré Time Crystal in Floquet-driven Rydberg Atomic Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27999)
*Shuai Shi,Dong-Yang Zhu,Yu Yang,Chu-Rong Pan,Jing-Wen Tang,Ya-Peng Zhang,Yan-Li Zhou,Wei-Tao Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Bang Liu,Dong-Sheng Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文报道了首次在实验上实现的莫尔时间晶体：通过双色驱动场激发里德堡原子系统，利用长程相互作用与耗散的协同效应，产生了具有拍频梳状结构的莫尔图案，表现为亚谐波周期性与基频的叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔时间晶体作为一种非平衡量子相，理论上由时间晶体的内禀振荡与另一频率相干干涉形成，具有超长拍周期的特征，但此前尚无实验实现。作者旨在填补这一实验空白。

Method: 对里德堡原子系统施加双色（双频）驱动光场，利用里德堡长程相互作用与耗散之间的竞争与协同，产生具有拍频梳特征的莫尔时间序。通过改变其中一路驱动频率，实验测量频谱中莫尔图案的交错演化，并系统扫描参数空间绘制相图。

Result: 实验观测到清晰的拍频梳状莫尔图案，确认了亚谐波周期性与基频叠加的特征。相图中标定出一个鲁棒区域，在该区域内莫尔时间序对激光失谐的扰动具有稳定性。

Conclusion: 该莫尔时间晶体为探索涌现的慢-快动力学和合成时空对称性提供了可控平台，也为驱动量子多体系统中复杂时间序的工程化开辟了新路径。

Abstract: A Moiré time crystal is a non-equilibrium quantum phase emerging from the coherent interference of two distinct frequencies, at least one being the intrinsic oscillation of a symmetry-broken time crystal. Its hallmark is an ultra-long beat period, reflecting a time-domain mapping of the Moiré fringes that arise from mismatched spatial lattices. However, to date, no experimental realization of such a Moiré time crystal has been reported. In this work, by applying a bichromatic driving field with two distinct frequencies, we demonstrate that the interplay between long-range Rydberg interactions and dissipation gives rise to a unique comb-like Moiré pattern characterized by a beat-note comb, which superimposes subharmonic periodicity and fundamental frequencies. This Moiré pattern formed by two mismatched drives is staggered in the spectrum as the frequency of one driver changes. We experimentally map the phase diagram of the system and identify a robust region where the Moiré temporal order persists against perturbations in laser detuning. The reported Moiré time crystal not only provides a controllable platform for exploring emergent slow-fast dynamics and synthetic space-time symmetries but also opens avenues for engineering complex temporal order in driven quantum many-body systems.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Scalable Graph Coreset Selection via Greedy Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27602)
*Zhaiming Shen,Alexander Cloninger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最小内积贪心选择规则的列选择图采样算法，仅需访问拉普拉斯矩阵的少量随机列子集，无需特征分解或全局遍历，适用于大规模图。在随机块模型下证明了算法的采样比例与簇大小成正比，且对带限图信号的均值估计误差随簇间连接减弱而衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图采样方法需要访问完整的图拉普拉斯矩阵，在大规模图上存储和计算代价过高，难以实际应用。因此需要一种无需完整拉普拉斯矩阵、无特征分解、适合大规模图的轻量级采样算法。

Method: 提出基于最小内积贪心规则的列选择图采样算法：每次迭代仅随机选取拉普拉斯矩阵的一小部分列，通过最小化内积的贪心准则选择代表性列（节点），避免特征分解和全图遍历。理论分析在随机块模型（SBM）下展开，并引入Paley-Wiener空间中的带限图信号框架进行误差分析。

Result: 理论证明：当度分布在节点间均衡时，算法采样比例与簇大小成正比；对带限图信号的均值估计误差受控，且随簇间连接减弱而衰减。合成数据和真实数据上的数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的列选择贪心采样算法在大规模图上兼顾了计算效率和采样质量，理论上可在随机块模型下保证采样代表性，实验也证实了其有效性，为大规模图信号处理提供了一种实用的采样方案。

Abstract: Sampling representative nodes from large graphs is fundamental to graph signal processing and network analysis, yet existing methods require access to the full graph Laplacian, making them impractical at scale. We propose a simple and effective column-selective graph sampling algorithm based on a minimum inner product greedy selection rule. At each iteration, the algorithm accesses only a small random subset of Laplacian columns, requiring no eigendecomposition or global graph traversal, making it well-suited for large-scale graphs where the full Laplacian cannot be stored in memory. We analyze the algorithm under the stochastic block model and show that, when the degree distribution is balanced across nodes, the algorithm achieves sampling proportional to cluster size, and that the resulting mean estimate is controlled for band-limited graph signals in the Paley-Wiener space, with the error decaying as inter-cluster connectivity weakens. Numerical experiments on both synthetic and real-world data validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>

### [122] [Relativistic transport near moving interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28569)
*Lorenzo Gavassino*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了相对论介质中平面运动界面的线性扰动，提出了一个基于拉普拉斯变换的传播子表示，将解表征为纯虚频率与波数模态的叠加，并给出了界面速度对这些模态的几何选择机制，最后在相对论流体力学和动理学理论中进行了应用说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一描述相对论介质中运动平面表面附近的线性局域扰动现象，包括边界层、尾流和激波渐近尾部等，这些表面可以是运动障碍物、热边界或外部源，需要一个统一的几何刻画框架。

Method: 采用拉普拉斯变换方法构造界面解的传播子（propagator）表示。核心在于证明解可分解为纯虚频率 iω 和纯虚波数 ik 的模态叠加，特定界面速度 v 对应的可容许模态由 {iω, ik} 平面上的直线 iω = v·ik 选取，当 v 变化时该直线扫过频谱空间。

Result: 得到了一个统一的几何描述：对于任意界面速度 v，界面局域解的可容许模态完全由频谱空间中直线 iω = v·ik 与系统谱的交点决定，从而给出了边界层、尾流和激波尾部等现象的统一传播子表示。

Conclusion: 该传播子表示和几何选取机制为相对论介质中运动界面附近的线性扰动提供了统一的理论框架，适用于相对论流体力学和动理学理论等多种物理系统。

Abstract: We study linear disturbances localized near planar surfaces moving at constant velocity $v$ in relativistic media. Depending on the physical setting, the surface may represent a moving obstacle, a thermal boundary, or an external source, providing a unified description of boundary layers, wakes, and the asymptotic tails of shock waves. The central result is a propagator representation of the interface solution that yields a geometric characterization of these phenomena. Using a Laplace-transform formulation, we show that the solution is a superposition of modes with purely imaginary frequency and wavenumber. For a given interface velocity, the admissible modes are selected by the line $iω=vik$ in the $\{iω,ik\}$ plane. As $v$ varies, this line sweeps across the spectrum, providing a unified geometric description of interface-localized solutions for arbitrary interface velocities. We illustrate the formalism with applications to relativistic hydrodynamics and kinetic theory.

</details>

### [123] [The Capacity Region of the Broadcast Channel with Non-Signaling Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27434)
*Yuhang Yao,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文完全刻画了在发送端和所有K个接收端可获得非信号（NS）辅助资源时，K用户离散无记忆广播信道的容量区域，该区域与Sato区域完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统广播信道容量区域的完全刻画是一个长期未解决的难题。作者旨在探索在非信号辅助这一新型合作机制下，广播信道容量区域是否存在简洁的闭合解。

Method: 通过引入非信号（non-signaling）辅助资源，将问题转化为：在每个接收端子集完全合作、并在与各边缘信道一致的最差联合信道律下，由所有子集和速率上界所定义的区域（即Sato区域），并证明该区域即为NS辅助下的容量区域。

Result: 证明了NS辅助下的K用户离散无记忆广播信道容量区域恰好等于Sato区域——由各消息子集的和速率上界定义，每个上界对应于该子集接收端完全合作时、在与各边缘信道一致的最差联合信道律下的最大和速率。

Conclusion: 非信号辅助资源使得广播信道的容量区域获得了简洁完整的刻画，表明Sato区域在NS辅助框架下具有确切的可达性，为理解广播信道的极限性能提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The capacity region of the $K$-user discrete memoryless broadcast channel is fully characterized when non-signaling (NS) assistance is available to the transmitter and all $K$ receivers. The NS-assisted capacity region is shown to coincide with Sato's region, i.e., the region defined by sum-rate bounds over all subsets of messages, where each bound corresponds to full cooperation among that subset of receivers under a worst-case joint channel law consistent with the marginal channels.

</details>

### [124] [Nonlinear Fourier spectral signatures of rogue waves observed in Bose-Einstein condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27734)
*Zhihao Zhang,Yankai Huang,Tiantian Li,Denglong Wang,Jie Peng*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文利用非线性傅里叶变换（NFT）和Zakharov-Shabat散射问题，研究了玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体系中两类一阶怪波的离散谱结构，揭示怪波形成的非线性光谱机制，并提出基于离散谱相位匹配的逆谱工程方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调制不稳定性框架已广泛用于解释连续背景上的怪波形成，但在零边界条件下的BEC物质波系统中，怪波的形成机制和非线性光谱结构仍未被充分探索。

Method: 基于聚焦非线性薛定谔方程的可积结构与Zakharov-Shabat散射问题，采用非线性傅里叶变换（NFT）进行非线性谱分析，结合Darboux重构，研究高斯波包诱导的极端局域化事件和实验观测到的Peregrine孤子。

Result: （1）高斯初始态：增大初始宽度导致离散本征值数量增加，从基孤子和束缚态过渡到圣诞树状怪波结构；（2）Peregrine孤子：局域扰动重塑离散谱构型和相位演化，实现多个束缚孤子模式的相干聚焦；（3）揭示了高阶怪波的光谱机制，并提出基于离散谱相位匹配的逆谱工程方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和控制零边界条件下物质波系统中的怪波形成提供了一种非线性谱视角，离散孤子模式的相干动力学是怪波形成的核心机制。

Abstract: Modulation instability provides an important framework for understanding rogue wave (RW) formation on continuous backgrounds. However, the formation mechanism and nonlinear spectral structures of RWs in Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) matter-wave systems with vanishing boundary conditions remain largely unexplored. Here, we employ the nonlinear Fourier transform (NFT), based on the integrable structure of the focusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation and the Zakharov-Shabat scattering problem, to investigate two representative classes of first-order RWs in BEC systems. Through nonlinear spectral analysis and Darboux reconstruction, we demonstrate that both Gaussian-wave-packet-induced extreme localization events and experimentally observed Peregrine solitons are governed by the coherent dynamics of discrete soliton modes encoded in the nonlinear spectrum. For Gaussian initial states, increasing the initial width leads to an increasing number of discrete eigenvalues, resulting in a transition from fundamental solitons and bound states to Christmas-tree-like RW structures. For experimentally observed Peregrine solitons, localized perturbations reshape the discrete spectral configuration and phase evolution, enabling coherent focusing of multiple bound soliton modes. Furthermore, we reveal the spectral mechanism of higher-order RWs and propose an inverse spectral-engineering approach based on discrete-spectrum phase matching. Our results provide a nonlinear spectral perspective for understanding and controlling RW formation in matter-wave systems with vanishing boundary conditions.

</details>


### [125] [Mean-Field Theory of Chiral Active Model B: Arrested Coarsening and Chiral Fingering Instabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27305)
*Kristian Blom,Uwe Thiele*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文从Wang等人引入的手性Ising模型的主方程出发，推导并分析了其平均场理论，揭示了手性偏置如何导致各向异性粗化、矩形畴结构以及手性诱导的停滞粗化，并在连续极限下得到了具有界面切向手性电流的活性场论，进而通过线性稳定性分析发现了手性指进不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wang等人提出的手性Ising模型展现了丰富的非平衡集体行为，但缺乏系统的平均场理论来描述其宏观动力学。本文旨在从微观主方程出发，通过系统粗粒化推导该模型的连续场论，从而揭示手性偏置如何在连续层面驱动系统远离平衡，并解释畴结构的各向异性粗化、停滞粗化及界面失稳等涌现现象。

Method: (1) 从2×2自旋块顺时针/逆时针旋转的主方程出发，推导空间离散的局域磁化强度演化方程；(2) 在离散层面分析手性偏置对各向异性粗化、矩形畴形成及停滞粗化的影响；(3) 取连续极限得到活性场论，其结构为Model-B型弛豫动力学加上一个使系统永久偏离平衡的手性电流项；(4) 粗粒化过程显式展示微观旋转偏置如何产生局域于界面的切向电流；(5) 基于连续理论对径向对称团簇进行线性稳定性分析，识别人手性指进不稳定性。

Result: (1) 离散层面：手性偏置导致畴的各向异性粗化，畴呈现近似矩形形状，并最终出现手性诱导的停滞粗化；(2) 连续场论具有Model-B结构加上手性电流项，手性电流永久驱动系统处于非平衡态；(3) 微观旋转偏置被显式粗粒化为局域在界面处的切向电流；(4) 线性稳定性分析揭示手性指进不稳定性：径向对称团簇的角向扰动被放大，最终导致径向不对称的旋转态或无序态。

Conclusion: 本文建立的手性Ising模型平均场理论成功连接了微观动力学与宏观行为，证明手性偏置通过界面局域的切向电流使系统永久偏离平衡，并导致各向异性粗化、停滞粗化以及手性指进不稳定性等丰富的非平衡现象。

Abstract: We derive and analyze a mean-field theory of the chiral Ising model recently introduced by Wang, Pietzonka, and Jülicher in "Edge Currents Shape Condensates in Chiral Active Matter", arXiv:2603.20064. Starting from the master equation for clockwise and counterclockwise rotations of 2x2 spin blocks, we first obtain spatially discrete evolution equations for the spatially resolved average magnetization. On this discrete level, we show that a chiral bias strongly affects phase coarsening: domains coarsen anisotropically, develop nearly rectangular shapes, and eventually display chirality-induced arrested coarsening. Taking the continuum limit of these equations yields an active field theory that has the structure of a relaxational Model-B-type dynamics supplemented by a chiral current that permanently drives the system out of equilibrium. The coarse graining explicitly shows how microscopic rotational bias generates tangential currents localized at interfaces. Using this continuum theory, we perform a linear stability analysis of radially symmetric clusters and identify a chiral fingering instability in which angular perturbations of the interface are amplified and eventually lead to radially asymmetric rotating states or disordered states.

</details>

### [126] [A Quantum Mechanical Approach to the Computation of Rovibrational Spectra of Diatomic Molecules in Strong Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27907)
*Nikhil Yenugu,Ashwani K Tiwari,Sangita Sen*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Wilson-Hamiltonian框架扩展至三维，用于计算双原子分子在强均匀磁场中的 rovibrational 光谱，给出了首个针对极端磁场下¹H₂的全量子力学计算结果，揭示了磁场引起的谱峰位移、分裂、合并等现象及其背后的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁场天体物理对象（如白矮星、中子星）的光谱解释缺乏实验数据，需要高分辨率、高可靠性的计算光谱。现有方法在强磁场下对电子-核运动与磁场的非微扰耦合处理不足。

Method: 扩展Wilson-Hamiltonian框架至三维，构建含场依赖的电子与核Hamiltonian，完整包含粒子运动与磁场的非微扰耦合；推导并实现了外磁场下rovibrational跃迁的电四极矩跃迁矩积分，严格遵循分子-场系统的选择定则。

Result: 获得了¹H₂在极端磁场下首个全量子力学rovibrational光谱计算结果；观察到随场强增大的谱峰位移、分裂、合并、出现和消失等现象；分别分析了电子与核的贡献，揭示了键的刚化、转动势垒的出现、场致态耦合/解耦以及转动态和振动态的对称性破缺等物理机制。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于所有场强下的双原子分子rovibrational光谱计算，精度足以用于实验解释，对高场光谱学和天体化学建模具有直接应用价值，既提供计算数据，也深化了对强磁场如何影响电子结构与核运动的理解。

Abstract: In the absence of experimental data for molecular spectra in strong magnetic fields, high resolution and reliable computational spectra are required for the interpretation of spectra collected from highly magnetic astrophysical objects. In this paper, we extend the Wilson-Hamiltonian framework, recently implemented and benchmarked by us (\textit{J. Chem. Theory Comput.}, \textbf{21}, 9753 (2025) ), to a general three-dimensional framework suitable for computing the rovibrational spectra of diatomic molecules in strong uniform magnetic fields. The field-dependent electronic and nuclear Hamiltonians capture full non-perturbative coupling between particle motion and the field making the method applicable to all field strengths. The electric quadrupole transition moment integrals for rovibrational transitions in external static magnetic fields are formulated, implemented, and computed to yield spectra which respect the selection rules of the molecule-field system. The spectral changes with increasing field strength such as shifting, splitting, merging, appearance and disappearance of peaks are noted. Contributions from electrons and nuclei are studied individually, as well as in unison to reveal the underlying physics such as stiffening of the bond, emergence of a rotational barrier, field-induced coupling/decoupling of states, and symmetry-breaking in rotational and vibrational states. These results provide the first fully quantum mechanical computational results for the rovibrational signature of $^1\mathrm{H}_{2}$ in extreme magnetic field environments with accuracy suitable for experimental interpretation. The methodology developed herein has direct relevance for high-field spectroscopy and astrochemical modeling, both for providing computational data as well as for understanding the spectral impact of strong magnetic fields on electronic structure and nuclear motion in molecules.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Computing Enabled ab initio Molecular Dynamics Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28548)
*Susanta Das,Subhamoy Bhowmik,Zhen Li,Milana Bazayeva,Danil Kaliakin,Akhil Shajan,Kenneth M. Merz*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了将量子测量（LUCJ ansatz）与样本基量子对角化（SQD）后处理相结合的量子-经典混合工作流，用于从头算分子动力学（AIMD），在气相和显式溶剂QM/MM模拟中均能以化学精度（1 kcal mol⁻¹以内）重现FCI参考结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子硬件在电子结构计算中的应用仍然面临精度和可扩展性挑战，尤其是在凝聚相分子动力学模拟中。作者旨在验证LUCJ+SQD这一量子-经典混合方案能否在实际分子动力学场景（包括QM/MM）中达到化学精度，从而为量子硬件集成到真实化学模拟提供可行路径。

Method: 采用化学启发的LUCJ（Local Unitary Cluster Jastrow）ansatz在量子设备上生成测量数据，随后通过样本基量子对角化（SQD）进行后处理，恢复行列式子空间并提取能量和解析核梯度。以STO-3G基组下的全组态相互作用（FCI）作为精确基准，在气相和显式溶剂QM/MM两种场景下进行基准测试，评估能量、梯度、AIMD轨迹稳定性及径向分布函数等结构量。

Result: 在气相基准测试中，SQD将FCI能量和梯度重现至1 kcal mol⁻¹以内，AIMD轨迹保持稳定；在显式溶剂QM/MM模拟中，SQD同样匹配了FCI的能量涨落和RMS梯度分布，并准确重现了溶质-溶剂径向分布函数。

Conclusion: LUCJ+SQD被确立为将当前量子硬件集成到QM/MM分子动力学中的实用路线，并首次展示了由量子电子结构引擎驱动的凝聚相QM/MM动力学模拟。

Abstract: We demonstrate a quantum-classical workflow for ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) in which quantum measurements from a chemistry-inspired LUCJ ansatz are post-processed using Sample-based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD) to recover determinant subspaces and deliver energies and analytical nuclear gradients for dynamics. As an exact benchmark, we use full configuration interaction (FCI) in the STO-3G basis, enabling a direct assessment of the accuracy of SQD. In gas-phase benchmarks, SQD reproduces FCI energies and gradients to within 1 kcal mol$^{-1}$ of the FCI reference and yields stable AIMD trajectories. In explicit-solvent QM/MM simulations, SQD retains this agreement, matching FCI energy fluctuations and RMS gradient profiles and reproducing solute-solvent structure as quantified by radial distribution functions. Overall, these benchmarks establish LUCJ+SQD as a practical route for integrating current quantum hardware into QM/MM molecular dynamics and provide an early demonstration of condensed-phase QM/MM dynamics driven by a quantum electronic-structure engine.

</details>

### [128] [Multibranched parametric resonance and swallowtail catastrophe in electromechanical oscillators with nonlinear friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27242)
*P. Y. Chan,L. Huang,X. Dong,M. I. Dykman,H. B. Chan*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示在微机械振荡器中，通过受控非线性摩擦可实现两对不同的周期-2态共存，其多稳态的起始由燕尾突变控制，拓展了参量共振的理解并建立了研究突变理论和多稳态非平衡动力学的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参量振荡器通常被认为仅具有一对相位相反的稳定周期-2态（双稳态），但单个振荡器中能否共存多对这样的态仍是悬而未决的问题。探究这一可能性对于拓展参量共振基础理论及其在自旋哈密顿量模拟、超导猫态制备等应用方面具有重要意义。

Method: 采用经典的受控非线性摩擦方法：利用驱动诱导的共振耦合，将振荡模式的两个振动量子转移到一个衰减更快的模式上。在微机械振荡器平台上进行实验验证，并结合理论分析，通过绘制分岔结构来定量刻画多稳态行为。

Result: 实验和理论均证实，受控非线性摩擦可诱导微机械振荡器中两对不同的周期-2态共存；多稳态的起始由燕尾突变（swallowtail catastrophe）控制，并定量绘制了相关的分岔结构图谱。

Conclusion: 研究结果拓宽了对参量共振的理解，确立了微纳机械振荡器作为研究突变理论和多稳态非平衡动力学的多功能平台的地位。

Abstract: Parametric resonance underpins the operation of a wide range of physical systems, from nanomechanical resonators to quantum-information systems and Ising machines. As an archetypal class of driven-dissipative systems, parametric oscillators are generally expected to exhibit a single pair of stable period-two states with opposite phases. This bistable behavior enables both the simulation of spin Hamiltonians and the preparation of superconducting cat states. Whether multiple pairs of such states can coexist in a single oscillator, however, remains an open question. Here, we show experimentally and theoretically that conventional controlled nonlinear friction can induce the coexistence of two distinct pairs of period-two states in a micromechanical oscillator. The friction is implemented via a canonical approach, utilizing a drive-induced resonant coupling that transfers two vibrational quanta from the oscillatory mode to a faster decaying mode. We demonstrate that the onset of multistability is governed by a swallowtail catastrophe and quantitatively map the associated bifurcation structure. Our results broaden the understanding of parametric resonance and establish micro- and nano-mechanical oscillators as a versatile platform for studying catastrophe theory and multistable nonequilibrium dynamics.

</details>

### [129] [Unification of Quantum Graph Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28024)
*Gian Luca Spitzer*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的量子集合子集定义，并以此为基础，统一地将经典图性质（如连通分量、独立集、团、着色）直接推广到量子图框架中。该方法恢复了已有的着色和连通分量定义，并对独立集和团提出了新变体，解决了现有定义中的一些反直觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图的许多性质（连通分量、独立集等）通过顶点子集来定义。然而，量子集合的天然子集概念过于僵化，导致这些性质向量子图的推广困难且零散，同一概念常存在多个不等价定义。作者旨在提出一个自然且动机良好的量子子集替代定义，以实现经典图性质向量子图的统一推广。

Method: 首先引入一种新的量子集合子集定义，该定义自然且具有良好的动机。基于此定义，作者将经典图性质的定义直接、统一地推广到量子图：将经典定义中的子集条件以直截了当的方式移植到量子框架中。

Result: 新框架成功恢复了量子着色和量子连通分量的已有定义。对于独立集和团，该方法给出了与现有定义不同但能解决其反直觉性质的新变体；同时，作者也展示了如何在自身框架中恢复一种重要的现有独立集定义。

Conclusion: 通过引入新的量子子集概念，作者提供了一种统一且自然的途径来定义量子图性质，弥合了经典图论与量子图论之间的概念鸿沟，并为独立集和团等概念提供了更合理的定义选项。

Abstract: Many properties of classical graphs are defined in terms of subsets of the vertex set. Examples include connected components, which are subsets $X \subseteq V(G)$ such that $X \times X^c$ and $E(G)$ are disjoint, or independent sets, for which $X \times X$ and $E(G)$ are disjoint. Direct generalisations of these definitions to quantum graphs are difficult to achieve, since the natural notion of subsets of a quantum set is much too rigid. As a consequence, approaches to generalising these classical properties to the quantum setting have been eclectic. In some cases, multiple inequivalent definitions of the same notion are in use. We introduce a natural and well-motivated alternative definition of subsets of a quantum set. Building on this, we propose unified definitions of quantum graph properties as straightforward generalisations of the classical definitions. We recover this way the established notions of colourings and connected components. For independent sets and cliques, our approach suggests variations that diverge from existing definitions, but address some of their counterintuitive properties. We nevertheless show how to recover an important existing definition of independent sets in our framework.

</details>
