### [170] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在标准单纯形上相对于负熵为L-光滑的凸函数类中，任意一阶方法在d = Ω(T²)时的收敛速率下界为Ω(L/T)，表明镜像下降在该类中至多对数因子意义下是最优的。这一非加速结果也扩展到了量子设定（von Neumann熵相对光滑）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明在相对光滑条件下加速可能不可能实现（Dragomir et al., 2022），但其构造的prox函数是病态的且与困难实例耦合。本文旨在证明：即使对于一个结构特别良好的具体prox函数（负熵），加速同样不可能实现，从而揭示相对光滑框架下加速的本质障碍。

Method: 作者采用下界构造方法，在d维标准单纯形上，针对负熵相对L-光滑的凸函数类，构造了一个困难实例，使得任何一阶方法在维度d = Ω(T²)的条件下，收敛速率下界为Ω(L/T)。通过信息论或oracle复杂性分析框架建立下界，并进一步将该构造推广到量子设定中的spectrahedron上。

Result: 证明了Ω(L/T)的收敛速率下界，与镜像下降仅差对数因子，从而确立了镜像下降在该设定下的近乎最优性。同时将该下界扩展至量子情形（Hermitian半正定迹为1矩阵空间上的von Neumann熵相对光滑函数类）。

Conclusion: 即使对于负熵这样结构优良的prox函数，相对光滑条件下的加速也是不可能的。这从本质上揭示了相对光滑与标准Lipschitz光滑之间的根本差异：ℓ₁-范数光滑可加速，而负熵相对光滑不可加速，原因在于单纯形的几何结构限制了算法的收敛行为。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Analytic Boundaries of Infinite-Spin-Tower Amplitudes from Hidden Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27300)
*Long-Qi Shao,Alessandro Vichi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了兼容幺正性、解析性、交叉对称性、多项式有界性以及"隐零"与相应分裂条件的亚纯振幅的一般形式。这类振幅被称为无限自旋塔（IST）振幅，由极点分布刻画。当极点无限均匀分布时，IST振幅退化为Veneziano振幅。作者以"纳入"（primal）方式构造了此类幺正振幅并解析地找到了边界，该边界小于但接近正定性边界。文中还论证了在无能级聚点的情况下，所推导的解析边界是亚纯振幅primal构造中的最大可能边界，并将此IST振幅推广到与Virasoro-Shapiro振幅相关的完全交叉对称情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索满足幺正性、解析性、交叉对称性等基本物理约束的亚纯振幅的普适形式，并确定其允许的参数空间边界，以约束紫外谱的行为。

Method: 采用"纳入"（primal）构造方法，基于幺正性、解析性、交叉对称性、多项式有界性以及隐零与分裂条件，构建无限自旋塔（IST）振幅，并解析推导允许区域的边界。

Result: 成功构造了幺正的IST振幅；当极点均匀分布时恢复Veneziano振幅；推导出的允许区域小于正定性边界（rule-out边界），但在无能级聚点时是primal构造中的最大可能边界；将IST振幅推广到完全交叉对称情形（与Virasoro-Shapiro振幅相关）；发现引力子极点对紫外谱施加幺正性约束。

Conclusion: IST振幅为理解满足基本物理约束的亚纯散射振幅提供了统一框架。primal构造所得到的解析边界接近但严格小于正定性边界，表明在无能级聚点时该边界已达最大。该框架可推广至包含引力子的完全交叉对称振幅，揭示了引力子极点与紫外谱幺正性之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We study the general form of meromorphic amplitudes that are compatible with unitarity, analyticity, crossing symmetry, polynomial boundedness and the hidden-zero and corresponding splitting conditions. These amplitudes are infinite-spin-tower (IST) amplitudes and are characterized by the distribution of poles. With infinitely many evenly separated poles, the IST amplitudes reduce to Veneziano amplitudes. We construct such unitary amplitudes in a primal way (rule in) and find the bounds analytically. The allowed region we derive is smaller than, but close to, the region allowed by the positivity bounds (rule out). We argue that, in the absence of an accumulation point in the energy levels, the analytic boundary we derive is the largest possible boundary in the primal construction of meromorphic amplitudes. This type of IST amplitude can also be extended to the fully crossing-symmetric case related to the Virasoro-Shapiro amplitude. We found from the IST amplitudes that graviton pole imposes unitarity constraints on UV spectrum.

</details>


### [172] [Wading the String Bog: CMB Birefringence in the Swampland](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27318)
*Guido D'Amico,Nemanja Kaloper,Alexander Westphal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出宇宙双折射观测可为沼泽地猜想提供直接实验检验。超轻标量场双折射机制受弱引力猜想的约束，若证实CMB双折射由超轻标量引起则会对沼泽地框架构成挑战。作者提出薄轴子畴壁可作为替代解释，并指出可通过双折射各向异性的探测来区分不同场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将弦理论中的沼泽地猜想（Swampland conjectures）与可观测宇宙学现象——宇宙微波背景（CMB）双折射联系起来，寻找对沼泽地框架的直接实验检验途径，弥合高能理论与观测宇宙学之间的鸿沟。

Method: 理论分析：首先论证超轻标量场导致双折射的机制受弱引力猜想（WGC）约束，使其参数空间受限；其次提出薄轴子畴壁（thin axionic domain walls）作为不依赖传播性超轻自由度的替代双折射机制；最后建议通过POLARBEAR、Simons Observatory和LiteBIRD等实验搜寻或排除双折射各向异性来区分两种场景。

Result: （1）弱引力猜想限制了超轻标量场双折射的参数空间；（2）若CMB双折射确由超轻标量引起，将对沼泽地框架构成挑战；（3）薄轴子畴壁无需传播性超轻自由度即可解释双折射，可化解上述困难；（4）双折射各向异性可作为区分两种场景的实验判据。

Conclusion: 宇宙双折射观测可作为沼泽地猜想的直接实验探针。不同理论场景（超轻标量 vs. 轴子畴壁）预言了不同的双折射各向异性特征，未来CMB偏振实验有能力对其进行甄别。作者还指出宇宙双折射可理解为一种类萨哈罗夫不对称性机制。

Abstract: We point out that observations of cosmic birefringence may provide direct experimental tests of Swampland conjectures. Ultralight scalar field birefringence mechanisms are constrained by Weak Gravity Conjecture, limiting their parameter space. Conversely, confirming that CMB birefringence is caused by ultralight scalars could spell problems for the Swampland framework. Resolving these difficulties without a revision of Swampland ideology points toward thin axionic domain walls, which can explain birefringence without propagating ultralight degrees of freedom. Importantly these conflicting scenarios for cosmic birefringence could be experimentally distinguished by a search for, or exclusion of, birefringence anisotropies, that could be measured by POLARBEAR, Simons Observatory and LiteBIRD. We also note that cosmic birefringence observables emerge via a Sakharov-like asymmetry, where linear polarization is first generated cosmologically and subsequently twisted by a parity-violating dynamics after last scattering.

</details>


### [173] [geoSCET: Soft Theorems from Power Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27322)
*Timothy Cohen,Patrick Hager,Andreas Helset*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将SCET框架应用于几何标量场理论，构建了"geoSCET"，揭示其软扇区中的涌现几何结构，并将几何软定理重新解释为有效场论幂次计数规则的直接推论，同时将结果推广至多重软辐射和圈图修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何软定理已在多个标量场理论中被发现，但其深层理论根源尚不清晰。作者旨在通过有效场论（SCET）的视角，揭示几何软定理的底层起源，即场空间微分同胚不变性，并为多重软辐射和圈阶修正提供系统性的组织框架。

Method: 将软共线有效理论（SCET）框架应用于几何标量场理论，构造"geoSCET"。通过分离硬模和软模，在软扇区中导出涌现的几何结构（与QCD SCET中涌现的软规范不变性类似）。利用有效场论的幂次计数规则，系统推导几何软定理，并推广至多重软发射和圈图修正情形。

Result: （1）geoSCET的软扇区自然涌现出与场空间微分同胚相关的几何结构；（2）几何软定理可直接由有效场论的幂次计数导出；（3）无势能的理论对任意数量的软发射满足普适的几何软定理，且对所有阶微扰论成立；（4）在软尺度引入势能不会破坏软物理的因子化结构。

Conclusion: 几何软定理的底层起源是场空间微分同胚不变性，geoSCET为此提供了系统且有力的理论框架，统一理解经典和量子层面的几何软行为。

Abstract: We apply the framework of Soft Collinear Effective Theory (SCET) to the theory of the geometric scalar field. The resulting ``geoSCET'' manifests an emergent geometry in the soft sector, mirroring the emergent soft gauge invariance of QCD SCET. We use geoSCET to derive the geometric soft theorems as straightforward consequences of effective-field-theory power counting, including extensions to multiple soft emissions and loop corrections. We demonstrate that theories without a potential satisfy universal geometric soft theorems for any number of soft emissions to all orders in perturbation theory. We also show that we can turn on a potential at the soft scale without spoiling the factorization of the soft physics. This work demonstrates the underlying field-space diffeomorphism origin of the geometric soft theorem.

</details>


### [174] [On quantum corrections to semiclassical strings on $\mathrm{AdS}_5\times S^5/\mathbb{Z}_{L}$ orbifold backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27327)
*Carlos Barredo Martínez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₅×S⁵/ℤ_L orbifold背景下的三种半经典弦解，对应𝒩=2箭图规范理论中的扭曲单迹算符。尽管缺乏最大超对称性，量子修正仍与扭曲Bethe ansatz的有限尺寸修正匹配。ℤ_L商允许弦具有分数缠绕数，产生了新的稳定半经典态子扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS/CFT框架下，研究orbifold背景中半经典弦解的量子修正，检验非最大超对称情形下Bethe ansatz与弦理论之间的对应关系是否仍然成立，并探索ℤ_L商引入的分数缠绕数所带来的新物理现象。

Method: 采用半经典展开方法，在AdS₅×S⁵/ℤ_L orbifold背景上构造SU(2)、SU(3)和SL(2)三个扇区的经典弦解，计算其能量的领头阶量子修正。将结果与对偶𝒩=2箭图规范理论中扭曲Bethe ansatz的连续极限（J→∞）有限尺寸修正及Landau-Lifshitz低能有效理论进行匹配。

Result: 1. 三个扇区中半经典弦解的领头阶量子修正与扭曲Bethe ansatz的有限尺寸修正完全匹配，表明即使缺乏最大超对称性，该对应关系依然成立；2. ℤ_L商使得弦在orbifold方向上可携带分数缠绕数μ，产生在AdS₅×S⁵中不存在的新型稳定半经典态子扇区；3. 这些稳定态的能量在μ→0极限下平滑连接到相关BPS扇区的态；4. 在“长箭图”机制L≫√λ≫1下，标准固定背景半经典展开被重新组织。

Conclusion: 本文验证了在𝒩=2 orbifold背景下，弦理论/Bethe ansatz对应关系在非最大超对称情形中依然稳健。分数缠绕数的引入丰富了半经典态的结构，出现了新的稳定子扇区。长箭图极限的分析表明该机制对半经典展开具有非平凡的重组效应，这对理解局域化结果具有重要意义。

Abstract: We consider three families of semiclassical string solutions on AdS$_5\times S^5/\mathbb{Z}_L$ orbifold backgrounds which correspond to `twisted' single-trace operators in the SU$(2)$, SU$(3)$ and SL$(2)$ sectors, respectively, of the dual 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ quiver gauge theory. The leading quantum correction to the classical energy of each solution is computed, and is matched, despite the lack of maximal supersymmetry, to the corresponding results from finite-size corrections to the twisted Bethe ansatz in the continuum $J\to\infty$ limit and the associated Landau-Lifshitz low-energy effective theory. The $\mathbb{Z}_L$ quotient allows the string to have fractional windings $μ$ along orbifolded directions, resulting in novel stable subsectors of semiclassical states that are absent in the analogous semiclassical string solutions on AdS$_5\times S^5$. The energies of these stable states connect smoothly to those of states in relevant BPS sectors as $μ\to0$. Motivated by recent localisation results on the dual gauge theory, we comment on the `long quiver' regime $L\gg\sqrtλ\gg 1$ from a semiclassical perspective, concluding that it reorganises the standard fixed-background semiclassical expansion.

</details>


### [175] [Holography in the linearized quantum gravity regime and modular crossed product](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27337)
*Avinandan Mondal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在半经典AdS/CFT对偶框架下，本文证明：对于边界上任意球状区域A，体态相对于AdS真空的相对熵与对偶CFT态的对应相对熵精确匹配（JLMS条件）；并利用模交叉积构造，严格证明了因果楔内局域半经典相干激发对应的CFT态熵满足真空减除的HRT公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AdS/CFT研究中，JLMS条件和HRT公式虽已被提出，但缺乏在纯AdS背景上线性化引力框架下的严格数学证明。作者旨在利用全息映射作为GNS希尔伯特空间的等距嵌入这一严格设定，为这两个核心结果提供坚实的数学基础。

Method: (1) 在半经典极限下，将体态取为纯AdS背景上的线性化度规扰动，满足线性化爱因斯坦方程；(2) 利用全息映射将体线性化引力的GNS希尔伯特空间等距嵌入到边界CFT的GNS希尔伯特空间，假设该映射保持真空态并支持AdS-Rindler楔重建；(3) 在编码子空间中，通过代数方法证明JLMS条件，即因果楔代数中相对熵的匹配；(4) 对因果楔内的局域半经典相干激发，运用模交叉积构造，将边界CFT的dressed Type-II代数中的态依赖熵严格推导至真空减除的HRT公式。

Result: (1) 成功证明：对于边界球状区域A，体态在因果楔代数中相对于AdS真空的相对熵，等于对偶CFT态在区域A的代数中相对于CFT真空的相对熵（JLMS条件）；(2) 对因果楔内对应于扰动几何的局域半经典相干激发，严格证明了dressed Type-II代数中CFT态的态依赖部分熵满足真空减除的HRT公式。

Conclusion: 在纯AdS背景线性化引力的严格设定下，JLMS条件和真空减除的HRT公式均可从全息映射的基本性质（等距嵌入、真空保持、楔重建）出发得到严格证明，模交叉积构造为处理Type-III代数中的熵定义问题提供了有效工具，进一步强化了AdS/CFT对偶中体-边界熵关系的数学基础。

Abstract: Within the semi-classical regime of AdS/CFT correspondence, we consider the limit where the bulk dynamical field is linearized metric perturbations satisfying linearized Einstein equations over background pure AdS spacetime. AdS/CFT correspondence gives us a holographic map, which is an isometric embedding map of the GNS Hilbert space of linearized gravity in the bulk (w.r.t. the AdS-invariant vacuum) to the GNS Hilbert space of CFT in the boundary (w.r.t. the Minkowski-invariant vacuum). We assume that the map takes AdS-vacuum in the bulk to CFT-vacuum in the boundary and that it allows AdS-Rindler wedge reconstruction. Then using this map, we show that for a given ball-shaped region in the boundary $A$, the relative entropy of a bulk state w.r.t. the AdS vacuum in the algebra of causal wedge associated to $A$ matches with the relative entropy of the dual CFT state w.r.t. the CFT vacuum in the algebra of CFT observables in $A$ in the code subspace, which is known as Jafferis-Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Suh (JLMS) condition. Furthermore, for localized semi-classical coherent excitations in the causal wedge associated to $A$ which corresponds to perturbed bulk geometry, we show rigorously using modular crossed product construction that the state-dependent part of entropy of the dual CFT state in the dressed Type-II algebra associated to $A$ satisfies vacuum subtracted Hubeney-Rangamani-Takayanagi (HRT) formula.

</details>


### [176] [NLSM amplitudes from a quartic two-derivative theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27345)
*Qu Cao,Zhenqi Han,Fan Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为SU(N) Goldstone玻色子散射的非线性σ模型(NLSM)引入了一种新的标量拉氏量表述，仅含单个四次两项导数相互作用项，可在所有圈阶重现平面NLSM振幅，揭示了该模型此前隐藏的简洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLSM虽为经典有效场论，但其传统表述包含无穷多个两项导数相互作用顶点，计算复杂度高。作者试图寻找一种更简洁的局域标量拉氏量形式，揭示NLSM隐藏的结构简单性，并提升高多重数振幅的计算效率。

Method: 引入一对携带相反"极性"的标量场ψ^±，构造仅含单个四次两项导数多项式算符的相互作用拉氏量。通过广义切割技术，在任意圈阶上证明该表述重现平面NLSM振幅，并展示Adler零点和前导双软因子在所有圈阶的显现性。

Result: 新表述在任意圈阶精确重现平面NLSM振幅；Feynman规则仅含单个四次顶点，Adler零点和前导双软因子在广义切割上对所有圈阶显现；显著提升了高多重数振幅的计算效率。此外，提出NLSM+φ^3理论可能存在类似的有限局域相互作用塔描述。

Conclusion: 通过仅含单个四次两项导数顶点的标量拉氏量，可完全捕捉平面NLSM的圈阶物理，揭示了该模型前所未有的简洁结构，并为其他有效场论的类似重构提供了思路。

Abstract: We revisit the well-known nonlinear sigma model (NLSM), an effective field theory describing the scattering of $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ Goldstone bosons and characterized by an infinite tower of two-derivative interactions. We introduce a local scalar Lagrangian involving two scalar fields $ψ^\pm$ carrying opposite "polarities", whose interacting part consists of a single polynomial quartic two-derivative operator, and prove that it reproduces planar NLSM amplitudes at all loop orders. This quartic two-derivative formulation reveals a previously hidden simplicity of the NLSM: its Feynman rules involve only a single quartic interaction vertex, making the Adler zero and the leading double-soft factor manifest at all loop orders on generalized cuts and significantly improving the efficiency of high-multiplicity computations. We also suggest a possible analogous description of the NLSM +$ φ^3 $ theory in terms of a finite tower of local interactions.

</details>


### [177] [Spectator Axions in String Inflation and Primordial Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27361)
*Michele Cicoli,Dario L. Lorenzoni,Evan McDonough,Francisco G. Pedro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究弦理论中轻 spectator 轴子对暴胀期间原初黑洞种子形成的影响，发现小衰变常数轴子对功率谱几乎无影响，而较大衰变常数轴子可增强扰动，实现"轴子辅助"原初黑洞产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初黑洞是暗物质的候选者，其产生的暴胀模型通常涉及普朗克尺度的场位移，需要紫外完备到量子引力。弦理论中的 Fibre Inflation 已被证明可产生 PBH 并满足 CMB 约束，但轴子动力学对原初扰动的影响尚不清楚，需系统研究以检验模型的鲁棒性并探索增强 PBH 种子机制的可能性。

Method: 在弦理论 Fibre Inflation 框架下，引入轻 spectator 轴子场，研究其动力学演化（包括场空间转弯等效应），计算轴子对曲率扰动功率谱的影响，系统扫描参数空间（衰变常数 f 和非微扰势前置因子），分析不同参数区域对原初扰动增强或抑制的行为。

Result: 发现两类行为：(1) 对于远小于普朗克质量的衰变常数 f ≪ M_Pl，轴子对曲率扰动功率谱影响可忽略，模型具有鲁棒性；(2) 对于较大但仍亚普朗克的衰变常数 f ≳ O(0.1) M_Pl，若非微扰势前置因子指数级小，则可显著增强扰动增长，降低实现 PBH 种子所需放大条件，即实现"轴子辅助的原初黑洞"机制。

Conclusion: Fibre Inflation 模型在大多数参数空间下对 spectator 轴子动力学具有鲁棒性；在特定参数区域，轴子可辅助增强原初扰动，为弦理论中 PBH 的产生提供了更丰富的唯象学与机制。

Abstract: We study the impact of light spectator axions on the seeding of primordial black holes (PBHs) during inflation in string theory. Primordial black holes exhibit unique and novel phenomenology, and may constitute the observed dark matter. Cosmic inflation provides a mechanism for producing them, but such inflation models typically feature Planckian field excursions, necessitating an ultraviolet completion into quantum gravity. String theory provides a natural framework for doing so, and indeed Fibre Inflation has been shown to produce PBHs while satisfying constraints from cosmic microwave background data. In this work we study the dynamics of axions during Fibre Inflation, and find a diverse and rich set of possibilities, including turns in field space and enhancement of primordial perturbations. We find that across most of parameter space, notably an axion with a far sub-Planckian decay constant $f\ll M_{\rm Pl}$, there is a negligible impact on the power spectrum of curvature perturbations, indicating an overall robustness of the model. On the other hand, an axion with a larger but still sub-Planckian decay constant, $f\gtrsim {\cal O}(0.1) M_{\rm Pl}$, and an exponentially small prefactor of its non-perturbative potential, can enhance the growth of perturbations, making it easier to achieve the amplification needed to seed PBHs, effectively realizing axion-assisted PBHs in string theory.

</details>


### [178] [A Darboux-Theorem-Based Derivation of Geometric Structures in Various Non-Abelian Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27417)
*Eugen-Mihaita Cioroianu,Stefan-Sabin Manolescu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统地将Faddeev-Jackiw方法应用于四类非阿贝尔规范理论（Freedman-Townsend、Yang-Mills、非阿贝尔BF模型及一个非线性BF型理论），统一推导了它们的相空间与辛结构、余迷向约束子流形、哈密顿量及约束的可约性，并结合狄拉克猜想重构了相应的拉格朗日规范变换及其可约性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为多种非阿贝尔规范理论提供一种统一、高效的框架，用以确定其哈密顿和拉格朗日规范结构，避免传统方法中繁琐的分步处理，同时清晰展示约束的可约性特征。

Method: 采用Faddeev-Jackiw辛方法，对每类理论依次确定相空间及其辛结构、余迷向约束子流形、哈密顿量以及约束的可约性；随后结合狄拉克猜想，直接计算重构对应的拉格朗日规范变换及其可约性结构。

Result: 成功获得了Freedman-Townsend模型、Yang-Mills理论、非阿贝尔BF模型及非线性BF型理论的完整哈密顿描述与拉格朗日规范对称性，统一展现了这些理论的约束可约性层级和规范变换的代数结构。

Conclusion: Faddeev-Jackiw方法为非阿贝尔规范理论提供了一条直接且统一的路径，能同时高效导出哈密顿和拉格朗日层面的规范结构，验证了该方法在处理含可约约束的复杂规范系统中的有效性和简洁性。

Abstract: This paper illustrates the straightforward application of the Faddeev--Jackiw approach to several non-Abelian gauge theories, namely, the Freedman--Townsend, Yang--Mills, and non-Abelian BF models, as well as a nonlinear theory of BF type. For each theory, the procedure determines the phase space and its symplectic structure, the coisotropic constraint submanifold, the Hamiltonian, and the reducibility properties of the constraints. By combining these results with the Dirac conjecture, one reconstructs through direct computation the corresponding Lagrangian gauge transformations and their reducibility structure. The analysis provides a unified and efficient derivation of the principal Hamiltonian and Lagrangian gauge structures of these non-Abelian theories.

</details>


### [179] [$w_{1+\infty}$ as the Frame Algebra of Kerr Soft Dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27469)
*Gabriel Menezes*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明Veneziano-Vilkovisky超平移产生一个由指数化软展开选择的塔结构，该结构生成三点Kerr多极矩。该塔具有宇称交替性，对于对齐自旋可用双曲积分求解至所有阶。手征投影后，其合成律给出经典w_{1+∞}括号，作者将此代数赋予物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Veneziano-Vilkovisky超平移（连接Bondi规范框架与散射体内禀框架的残余大微分同胚）的物理内涵，特别是它与渐近对称代数w_{1+∞}之间的联系。

Method: 从Veneziano-Vilkovisky超平移出发，利用指数化软展开 e^{ηωa·q} 的偶/奇分解分析塔结构的宇称交替性；对于对齐自旋情形，通过双曲积分求解塔结构至任意阶；进一步进行手征投影，推导其合成律。

Result: 该塔结构由指数化软展开选择，在三点情形下生成Kerr多极矩；塔结构呈现宇称交替；对齐自旋时可借助双曲积分求解至所有阶；手征投影后的合成律即为经典w_{1+∞}括号。

Conclusion: 经典w_{1+∞}代数的物理内容被赋予为Veneziano-Vilkovisky超平移所生成塔结构的手征投影合成律，从而建立了残余大微分同胚与渐近对称代数之间的直接联系。

Abstract: The Veneziano--Vilkovisky supertranslation is the residual large diffeomorphism relating the canonical Bondi frame to the intrinsic frame of the scattering bodies. We show it leads a tower selected by the exponentiating soft expansion, the object generating the Kerr multipoles at three points. Since \(e^{ηωa\cdot q}\) splits into even and odd parts, the tower alternates parity, and for aligned spin we solve it to all orders in hyperbolic integrals. After a chiral projection its composition law is the classical $w_{1+\infty}$ bracket: the physical content we assign to that algebra.

</details>


### [180] [Kerr Soft Dressing and the $w_{1+\infty}$ Frame Algebra at Null Infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27478)
*Gabriel Menezes*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了与Kerr选择软 dress 相关的电荷生成的内禀/规范标架字典，从VV超平移出发，将高自旋问题表述为零无穷远的逆问题。通过将软核与Kerr选择的指数化投影匹配，确定了生成元的一个宇称分量，并利用螺旋度共轭确定另一半。该方案在领头阶重现VV超平移，在次领头阶固定了光滑广义BMS矢量的旋度而非散度。匹配的物理原因在于相同的指数化软因子是GOV自旋三点算符的经典极限，生成了Kerr多极矩塔。文章进一步解释了对应的硬通量荷，展示了其外态作用如何给出软定理的Ward表示，并揭示了T*S²上的多项式Poisson代数（含局域w_{1+∞}型约化）作用于这些标架变换生成元，但其不对Kerr选择数据单独闭合，从而阐明了w_{1+∞}类结构在Kerr黑洞散射中的物理角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解Kerr黑洞散射中软定理与渐近对称性的深层联系，需要构建一个系统性的框架，将高自旋软因子与Kerr多极矩结构统一起来，并澄清w_{1+∞}类代数结构的物理意义——它并非Kerr数据的对称性，而是改变内禀/规范标架的变换。

Method: 从VV超平移出发，将高自旋问题表述为零无穷远（null infinity）上的逆问题：构建由宇称适配的最大纵向标量χ_t^(s)构成的软核K_{AB}^{(s,0)}[t]，将其与Kerr选择的指数化普适软贡献投影进行匹配，从而逐级确定生成元t^{A_1…A_s}的一个宇称分量。螺旋度共轭关系（指数化源满足S_{+,exp}的复共轭等于(-1)^s S_{-,exp}）固定另一半：偶数阶为电投影，奇数阶为磁投影。在次领头阶，该方案固定的是广义BMS矢量的旋度而非散度。进一步分析硬通量荷的外态作用，给出软定理的Ward表示，并考察T*S²上多项式Poisson代数（含局域w_{1+∞}型约化）在这些生成元上的作用。

Result: （1）成功构建了Kerr选择的内禀/规范标架字典，偶数阶对应Kerr质量矩（电型），奇数阶对应Kerr流矩（磁型），与Kerr多极矩的交替结构一致；对齐自旋情形下投影是穷尽的，可以闭式求解整个塔。（2）领头阶重现VV超平移，次领头阶固定光滑广义BMS矢量的旋度而非散度。（3）匹配的物理根源在于相同的指数化软因子既是GOV自旋三点算符的经典极限，又生成Kerr多极矩塔。（4）T*S²上的多项式Poisson代数（含局域w_{1+∞}约化）作用于这些标架变换生成元，但不对Kerr选择数据单独闭合，其物理角色是移动内禀/规范字典，可观测印记从s=0的位移记忆和s=1的自旋记忆开始，延伸到更高阶的电、磁记忆矩。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个自洽的框架，将Kerr黑洞的高自旋软因子、多极矩结构与渐近对称性统一起来。核心结论是：w_{1+∞}类结构并非Kerr散射的守恒对称代数，而是改变内禀/规范标架的变换，其可观测效应表现为各级记忆效应（位移记忆、自旋记忆及更高阶电磁记忆矩）。该框架为理解黑洞散射中的红外结构与渐近对称性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We construct the charge-generated intrinsic/canonical frame dictionary associated with the Kerr-selected soft dressing. Starting from the VV supertranslation, we formulate the higher-spin problem as an inverse problem at null infinity: the soft kernel ${\mathcal K}^{(s,0)}_{AB}[t]$, built from the parity-adapted maximally longitudinal scalar $χ^{(s)}_t$, is matched to the Kerr-selected exponentiating projection of the universal soft contribution, thereby determining one parity component of the generator $t^{A_1\cdots A_s}$. Helicity conjugation fixes which one: the exponentiating source obeys $\overline{S^{(s)}_{+,{\rm exp}}}=(-1)^sS^{(s)}_{-,{\rm exp}}$, so the tower fixes the electric projection of the source at even levels and the magnetic projection at odd ones, matching the alternation of the Kerr mass and current moments; for aligned spin the projection is exhaustive and we solve the tower in closed form. The prescription reproduces the VV supertranslation at leading order and fixes the curl, not the divergence, of a smooth generalized-BMS vector at subleading order. The reason for the matching is physical: the same exponentiating soft factor is the classical limit of the Guevara--Ochirov--Vines spinning three-point operator and generates the Kerr multipole tower. We explain the corresponding hard flux charges and show how their external-state action gives the Ward representation of the soft theorem. The polynomial Poisson algebra on $T^\ast S^2$, with local $w_{1+\infty}$-type reductions, then acts on these frame-changing generators; it does not close on the Kerr-selected data alone. This gives the physical role of the $w_{1+\infty}$-like structure in Kerr black-hole scattering: it moves the intrinsic/canonical dictionary. Its observable imprint begins with displacement memory at $s=0$ and spin memory at $s=1$, followed by higher electric and magnetic memory moments.

</details>


### [181] [Non-relativistic Floquet Conformal Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27668)
*Diptarka Das,Sumit R. Das,Arnab Kundu,Krishnendu Sengupta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了具有非相对论共形不变性的系统在周期性驱动（Floquet动力学）下的理论框架，揭示了由双曲、椭圆和抛物线过渡面分隔的两种动力学相，并指出了在幺正费米气体等实验系统中的可实现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索周期性驱动下具有非相对论共形不变性的多体系统的动力学行为，寻找普适的动力学相分类，并建立与全息对偶中时空几何的对应关系。

Method: 发展Floquet动力学的理论形式；通过计算驱动过程中共形生成元在基态中的期望值以及基态保真度，分析其频闪演化行为；利用全息对偶将动力学相映射到体时空中的几何结构（稳态极限面、极端Killing视界）。

Result: 发现两种动力学相——双曲相（频闪观测量指数增长/衰减）和椭圆相（振荡行为），两者由抛物线过渡面分隔（幂律行为）。双曲相对应体时空中的类时稳态极限面（如能层），抛物线相对应极端Killing视界。

Conclusion: 该分析完全普适，可直接应用于幺正极限附近的束缚费米子和共振任意子系统，前者可为这些动力学相提供实验验证。全息视角进一步揭示了动力学相与时空几何的深层联系。

Abstract: We develop a formalism for studying Floquet dynamics for systems with {\it non-relativistic} conformal invariance in $d$ spatial dimensions. Our analysis indicates the existence of two dynamical phases, hyperbolic and elliptic, separated by a parabolic transition surface. We demonstrate this by studying the expectation value of a conformal generator in the many body ground state during the drive, as well as the fidelity of this state. Stroboscopically, they behave exponentially in the hyperbolic phase, show oscillatory behavior in the elliptic phase, and exhibit power-law on the transition surface. Our analysis is completely universal and can be directly applied to several systems including trapped fermions near unitarity and resonant anyons. The former can provide experimental signatures of these dynamical phases. We also comment on a holographic perspective of such driven non-relativistic CFTs and demonstrate that the hyperbolic phase is associated with a timelike stationary-limit surface, such as an ergosphere, in the bulk, while the parabolic phase corresponds to an extremal Killing horizon.

</details>


### [182] [Vacuum fluctuations in Rainbow space-time: Study of Casimir effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27722)
*Bhagya. R,Suman Kumar Panja*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究了彩虹时空中的卡西米尔效应，通过格林函数方法计算了修正的卡西米尔能量和力，发现不同彩虹函数选择会导致卡西米尔力增大、减小或不变，并对彩虹参数给出了10⁻²⁴量级的实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将卡西米尔效应推广到彩虹时空（一种基于 doubly special relativity 的量子引力唯象模型）背景下，探究 Planck 尺度物理对卡西米尔能量和力的修正，并利用实验数据约束彩虹参数。

Method: 从彩虹时空中标量场的拉格朗日量出发，以 delta 函数势引入平行板；将能动张量的真空期望值表示为作用于格林函数的二次算子；求解彩虹时空中标量场的 Euler-Lagrange 方程获得格林函数解，进而得到修正的卡西米尔能量和力表达式。

Result: 对于两类彩虹函数，卡西米尔能量和力的绝对值分别减小和增大；对于某一特定彩虹函数选择，结果与标准 Minkowski 时空一致。与实验测量的卡西米尔力比较，给出彩虹参数依赖项的约束上界约为 10⁻²⁴ 量级。

Conclusion: 彩虹时空的量子引力效应会对卡西米尔效应产生可计算的修正，不同类型的彩虹函数导致不同的修正行为，当前实验精度已将彩虹参数限制在极小的尺度（~10⁻²⁴），表明 Planck 尺度修正极其微弱。

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect in rainbow space-time, focusing on leading-order corrections to the Casimir energy and force. Starting with the scalar field Lagrangian in rainbow space-time, with parallel plates introduced through delta-function potentials, we find the corresponding energy-momentum tensor. We obtain the vacuum expectation value of this energy-momentum tensor by expressing it as a quadratic operator acting on the Green's function. By solving the Euler-Lagrange equation of a scalar field in rainbow space-time, we obtain the Green's function solutions. Employing these Green's function solutions in the vacuum expectation value of the energy-momentum tensor, we obtain the modified Casimir energy and Casimir force expressions in rainbow space-time. We study the variation of the deformed Casimir force and energy with the distance between the plates for different choices of rainbow functions. Our results show that for two choices of rainbow functions, the absolute value of the Casimir energy and force is decreasing or increasing, whereas for one specific choice of rainbow functions, it remains the same as the standard result in Minkowski space-time. Comparing our result with experimentally measured value of Casimir force, we obtain the bound on the rainbow parameter dependent terms to be of the order of 10^-24.

</details>


### [183] [A comparison of two constructions for dynamical corrections to Wald entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27732)
*Sayantani Bhattacharyya,Parthajit Biswas,Nilay Kundu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对比了两种将稳态黑洞 Wald 熵推广至非稳态情形的构造方法：基于近邻视界 boost 对称性的 $S_\text{Wall}$ 和基于协变相空间 Wald-Iyer Noether 荷的 $S_\text{dyn}$，阐明了两者的本质差异，并建立了从 $S_\text{dyn}$ 逆向推导 $S_\text{Wall}$ 的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高阶导数引力理论中，Wald 熵仅适用于稳态黑洞，将其推广至动力学（非稳态）情境存在不同方法论路径。两种近期构造 $S_\text{Wall}$ 与 $S_\text{dyn}$ 在出发点和适用范围上存在显著差异，厘清二者的联系与区别对理解动力学黑洞熵的本质至关重要。

Method: 通过对比两种构造的技术结构——$S_\text{Wall}$ 利用近邻视界 boost 对称性且天然包含动力学涨落的高阶项，$S_\text{dyn}$ 基于 Wald-Iyer Noether 荷的协变相空间分析且仅定义于稳态背景的线性化涨落——分析二者的方法论差异；开发算法实现从已知 $S_\text{dyn}$ 表达式反推出 $S_\text{Wall}$ 的局域表达式，并以 Riemann-squared 高阶导数引力为例进行显式验证。

Result: (1) 从 $S_\text{Wall}$ 得到 $S_\text{dyn}$ 是直接的，但反向推导更为微妙；(2) 在满足特定技术条件下，给出了从 $S_\text{dyn}$ 出发构造 $S_\text{Wall}$ 局域表达式的算法；(3) 在 Riemann-squared 高阶导数引力实例中验证了上述分析结论。

Conclusion: $S_\text{Wall}$ 与 $S_\text{dyn}$ 虽均旨在推广 Wald 熵，但基于截然不同的方法论基础。$S_\text{Wall}$ 构造上更具一般性（不依赖线性化），而 $S_\text{dyn}$ 框架更贴近标准协变相空间形式。二者之间的转换在特定条件下可通过系统算法实现。

Abstract: In this work, we analyze the differences and similarities between two recent constructions, which are distinct in their methodologies for extending the Wald entropy of stationary black holes to non-stationary situations in general higher-derivative gravity. One of them, denoted by $S_\text{Wall}$, is constructed by exploiting the boost symmetry of the near-horizon geometry, whereas the other, denoted by $S_\text{dyn}$, is obtained from a covariant phase-space analysis based on the Wald-Iyer Noether charge formalism. While $S_\text{dyn}$ is, by construction, defined only for linearized fluctuations around a stationary black hole solution, $S_\text{Wall}$ does not require such a linearization for its construction. Although the linearization is necessary to interpret $S_\text{Wall}$ as a well-defined notion of entropy, the construction itself naturally contains terms that are higher order in the dynamical fluctuations. By comparing the technical structures underlying the two constructions, we clarify the fundamental differences between the methods on which they are based. We demonstrate that while the construction of $S_\text{dyn}$ given the $S_\text{Wall}$ is straightforward, the converse is more subtle. We develop an algorithm to obtain a local expression for $S_\text{Wall}$ from a known expression for $S_\text{dyn}$ in a generic diffeomorphism-invariant theory of gravity, provided certain technical conditions are satisfied. We justify our analytical findings with explicit demonstrations in a particular case: the Riemann-squared example of the higher-derivative theory of gravity.

</details>


### [184] [Non-conformal obstructions to bubble expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27874)
*David Mateos,Mikel Sanchez-Garitaonandia,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究了非共形热力学下一阶相变膨胀气泡的流体动力学，揭示了气泡壁和流体流动中的新流体动力学障碍，发现了"激波爆轰"新解，表明这些效应显著约束了气泡壁速度空间并可能抑制引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有气泡膨胀研究多基于袋模型（bag-model）等共形热力学假设，但实际物理系统（如QCD-like理论）具有非共形热力学行为。作者旨在系统研究非共形热力学对一阶相变气泡膨胀流体动力学的影响，以更准确地建模相变并预测引力波谱。

Method: 构建一类在袋模型与QCD-like理论之间插值的状态方程；以纯SU(3) Yang-Mills理论为基准确定气泡解；通过流体动力学分析发现新的障碍和新解类别；利用全息对偶展示插值可实现性；代码已公开。

Result: （1）发现了气泡壁处和流体流动中的新型流体动力学障碍，可部分或完全消除原本允许的解；（2）发现新解类"激波爆轰"——在稀疏波中插入额外激波的普通爆轰；（3）气泡壁速度空间被显著约束，不同膨胀区间出现间隙，QCD-like理论中所有爆轰解（含激波爆轰）均被排除；（4）这些效应强烈影响流体动能预算，可能压制最高效的引力波产生构型。

Conclusion: 非共形动力学对准确建模相变及引力波谱至关重要，忽略这些效应可能导致对引力波信号的高估。代码公开，并展示了通过全息方法实现袋模型与QCD-like极限之间插值的途径。

Abstract: We investigate the hydrodynamics of expanding bubbles in first-order phase transitions with non-conformal thermodynamics. We analyze a broad class of equations of state interpolating between bag-model descriptions, commonly used for electroweak transitions, and QCD-like theories. As a concrete benchmark, we determine the bubble solutions for pure SU(3) Yang-Mills theory. We uncover a new set of hydrodynamic obstructions to bubble expansion. These obstructions arise both at the bubble wall and along the fluid flow, and can partially or completely eliminate otherwise allowed solutions. We also identify a new class of solutions, which we dub "shocked detonations", consisting of ordinary detonations with an additional shock inserted in the rarefaction wave. As a consequence of these obstructions, the space of admissible bubble wall velocities is significantly constrained, with gaps appearing between different expansion regimes. For example, for QCD-like theories, all detonation solutions, including shocked detonations, are excluded. We show that these effects can strongly impact the kinetic energy budget of the fluid and, therefore, the resulting gravitational-wave signal, potentially suppressing the most efficient configurations. Our results highlight the importance of non-conformal dynamics for accurately modeling phase transitions and the resulting gravitational-wave spectrum. The code used to construct the bubble solutions is publicly available. For completeness, we also show how the interpolation between the bag-model and QCD-like limits can be realized holographically by varying the backreaction of matter fields on the geometry.

</details>


### [185] [A regulated zero-temperature construction of the Fundamental Modular Region in pure Yang--Mills theory and QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27896)
*Rodrigo Carmo Terin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出将基础模区域(FMR)表述为带规制的副本加权Landau规范在零温极限下的结果。有限β时测度局域在绝对极小值上，领头阶修正由逆Faddeev-Popov算子主导。通过小SU(2)基准测试的群体退火和集体盆地跳跃算法实现了数值验证。该构造无需参数化FMR边界即可定义绝对Landau规范，并自然推广到QCD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点规范理论中Gribov拷贝问题——传统Landau规范固定存在多个规范等价拷贝，需挑选绝对极小值来定义无歧义的非微扰规范固定。现有方法往往需参数化FMR边界，推广到QCD困难。

Method: 将FMR定义为带副本权重的Landau规范在零温(β→∞)极限下的测度支撑集。有限β时，引入Boltzmann因子使测度局域在绝对极小值附近，领头阶修正由逆FP算子特征谱决定。数值上采用群体退火与集体盆地跳跃算法在SU(2)上进行计算验证。

Result: 该方法成功构建了绝对Landau规范而不需显式参数化FMR边界。小SU(2)数值基准测试验证了方案的可行性，并给出FMR上规范固定真空波泛函的Hamilton量诠释。

Conclusion: 该构造为无歧义的非微扰Landau规范固定提供了新框架，避免了FMR边界参数化的困难，可自然推广到完整QCD，并建立了与真空波泛函的联系。

Abstract: We formulate the Fundamental Modular Region (FMR) as the zero-temperature limit of regulated copy-weighted Landau gauges. At finite $β$, the measure localizes on absolute minima, and the leading correction is dominated by the inverse Faddeev--Popov (FP) operator. A small $SU(2)$ benchmark provides a computational realization through population annealing and collective basin hopping. Our construction defines absolute Landau gauge without parametrizing the FMR boundary and extends naturally to full quantum chromodynamics (QCD). It also admits a Hamiltonian interpretation in terms of the gauge-fixed vacuum wave functional on the FMR.

</details>


### [186] [Generalized Kazakov-Migdal Models on Graphs via Artin-Ihara $L$-function and Random Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27935)
*So Matsuura,Kazutoshi Ohta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在图上引入了一类基于Artin-Ihara L-函数的Kazakov-Migdal型规范理论，将圈图上的模型重构为受Schur测度支配的随机分区模型，在大Nc极限下精确求解，揭示了Gross-Witten-Wadia相变与Young图边界触碰、玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚之间的深刻联系，并阐明了强弱对偶的组合解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一描述此前文献中提出的各类Kazakov-Migdal型图规范理论模型，并利用调和分析工具深入理解其相变机制与对偶性的本质。

Method: 通过Artin-Ihara L-函数在图上定义KM型规范理论；利用群流形上的调和分析将圈图上的模型重构为Schur测度支配的随机分区模型；在大Nc极限下基于基础表示精确求解该随机分区模型，并从谱曲线出发推导droplet图像，建立酉矩阵特征值密度与随机分区Maya图密度之间的对应关系。

Result: （1）Gross-Witten-Wadia相变恰好在Young图的极限形状触碰允许表示空间边界时发生；（2）该相变与玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚密切相关；（3）强弱耦合对偶性具有自然的组合解释——即Young图与其补图之间的交换，这反映了Artin-Ihara L-函数的函数方程；（4）建立了酉矩阵特征值密度与Maya图密度之间的明确关系。

Conclusion: 基于Artin-Ihara L-函数的框架成功统一了图上的KM型规范理论，揭示了其中相变、对偶性与组合结构之间的内在联系，为理解该类模型的非微扰性质提供了精确可解的新视角。

Abstract: We introduce Kazakov-Migdal (KM)-type gauge theories on graphs via the Artin-Ihara $L$-function, providing a unified description of the models proposed in prior works. Using harmonic analysis on the group manifold, we reformulate the KM-type model on the cycle graph as a random partition model governed by the Schur measure. We exactly solve the KM-type model on the cycle graph in the fundamental representation as the random partition model in the large $N_c$ limit and demonstrate that the Gross-Witten-Wadia phase transition occurs precisely when the limiting shape of the Young diagram touches the boundary of the allowed representation space. We further clarify that this phase transition is intimately related to Bose-Einstein condensation, and the strong/weak coupling duality possesses a natural combinatorial interpretation as the exchange between the Young diagram and its complement, reflecting the functional equation of the Artin-Ihara $L$-function. We also establish a definitive relationship between the eigenvalue density of the unitary matrix and the Maya diagram density of the random partitions by deriving a droplet picture from the spectral curve.

</details>


### [187] [Mega-Space Current Algebra and Green-Schwarz Geometry in Heterotic String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27988)
*Machiko Hatsuda,Shin Sasaki,Masaya Yata*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在显式T-对偶框架下研究杂化弦理论中的Lorentz联络与规范联络，核心动机是建立挠率Lorentz联络与Yang-Mills联络之间的Bergshoeff–de Roo型平行关系。作者在一个同时纳入Lorentz和规范扇区的"超大空间"中构造世界面流代数，通过推广Poláček-Siegel方案计算扩展生成元的对易子，确定广义联络，并证明该流代数将杂化Chern-Simons结构几何地嵌入广义标架与曲率中。此外，弦协变导数（"nachos"算子）代数的Jacobi恒等式自然导出Green-Schwarz反常相消条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是在显式T-对偶框架下建立杂化弦α'修正中挠率Lorentz联络与Yang-Mills联络之间的Bergshoeff–de Roo型平行关系，从而揭示两种联络在高阶修正中的统一几何结构。

Method: 作者构造了一个同时包含Lorentz和规范扇区的"超大空间"（mega-space），在该空间中建立世界面流代数；推广Poláček-Siegel方案，计算扩展生成元的对易子以确定广义联络；通过"nachos"算子（弦协变导数）的代数结构，将杂化Chern-Simons项几何嵌入广义标架和曲率中。

Result: （1）超大空间流代数将杂化Chern-Simons结构成功嵌入广义标架与曲率之中；（2）"nachos"算子代数的Jacobi恒等式直接给出Green-Schwarz反常相消条件，表明该条件是弦协变导数代数自洽性的必然结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，杂化弦的Lorentz与规范联络的统一可以通过T-对偶协变的超大空间流代数实现，Bergshoeff–de Roo平行性在此框架中得到几何诠释，且Green-Schwarz反常相消机制根植于弦协变导数代数的Jacobi恒等式，深化了对杂化弦α'修正几何结构的理解。

Abstract: We study the Lorentz and gauge connections in heterotic string theories within a framework of manifest T-duality. A central motivation is the Bergshoeff--de Roo (BdR) type parallelism between the torsionful Lorentz and Yang-Mills connections in heterotic $α'$-corrections. We formulate worldsheet current algebras in a mega-space that incorporates the Lorentz and gauge sectors simultaneously. By generalizing the Poláček-Siegel scheme, we evaluate the commutators of the extended generators and determine the generalized connections. We show that this mega-space current algebra geometrically embeds the heterotic Chern--Simons structures into the generalized vielbeins and curvatures. We also show that the Green-Schwarz anomaly cancellation condition follows from the Jacobi identity for the algebra of stringy covariant derivatives, the ``nachos'' operators $\triangleright_{\mathcal{M}}$.

</details>


### [188] [Quantum Trigonometric Spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider Models from $K$-theoretic Coulomb Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28043)
*Gleb Arutyunov,Lukas Hardi,Rob Klabbers*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用4d N=2项链箭图规范理论的K理论库仑分支，对具有ℓ个自旋态、N个粒子的三角自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型进行量子化。核心工具是由minuscule电荷的阿贝尔化磁单极算子导出的L算子代数，该代数将项链箭图转化为可积自旋链。最低哈密顿量对应于水平量子圈代数量子行列式的第一模式，其Bethe子代数生成极大族对易哈密顿量。文中还推导了量子化物理自旋变量的对易关系和量子运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的三角自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型进行量子化，并利用规范理论/可积系统的对应关系，通过K理论库仑分支的代数结构为量子可积模型提供严格的代数框架。

Method: 采用4d N=2项链箭图（ℓ个节点，秩为N）的K理论库仑分支描述经典模型；构建由minuscule电荷的阿贝尔化磁单极算子导出的L算子代数；将该代数转化为可积自旋链，生成一族对易哈密顿量；利用水平量子圈代数的量子行列式及Bethe子代数构造极大对易族。

Result: (1) 最低哈密顿量与K理论库仑分支代数内水平量子圈代数的量子行列式第一模式一致；(2) Bethe子代数生成极大族对易哈密顿量；(3) 导出了量子化物理自旋变量的对易关系及量子运动方程。

Conclusion: 通过规范理论的K理论库仑分支，成功实现了三角自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的量子化，建立了量子可积模型与项链箭图规范理论之间的深刻对应，为量子可积系统的代数结构研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: We quantize the trigonometric spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider model of $N$ particles each with $\ell$ spin states using the recently developed description of the classical model in terms of the $K$-theoretic Coulomb branch of the 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ quiver gauge theory for the necklace quiver with $\ell$ nodes of rank $N$. The main algebraic tool is an algebra of $L$-operators derived from abelianized monopole operators of minuscule charge, which turns the necklace quiver into an integrable spin chain by producing a family of commuting Hamiltonians. We show that the lowest Hamiltonian coincides with the first mode of the quantum determinant of the horizontal quantum loop algebra living inside the $K$-theoretic Coulomb branch algebra, whose Bethe subalgebra generates a maximal family of commuting Hamiltonians. Finally, we derive the commutation relations and quantum equations of motion of the quantized physical spin variables.

</details>


### [189] [Interior zeros of supersymmetric indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28143)
*Yu Nakayama,Tadashi Okazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 超对称指数的内部零点与超对称 zeta 函数中算术系数 δ(ν) 的指数增长直接相关，该增长率由最近零点决定。这些零点可从有限项 q-级数展开中检测，并阻碍自由或 s-禁闭红外描述。在巨引力子展开框架下，内部零点表现为有限 N 效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超对称指数在单位圆盘内（|q|<1）出现零点的充要条件，并将其与红外物理（如禁闭或自由相）关联起来，同时理解内部零点在引力对偶（巨引力子展开）中的物理意义。

Method: 基于超对称 zeta 函数的解析结构，建立算术系数 δ(ν) 的渐近增长与最近零点位置的等价关系；通过有限项 q-级数系数提取 δ(ν)，从而检测内部零点；将这一判别方法应用于 4d N=1 SU(2) SQCD，并结合巨引力子展开分析 N=4 U(N) Schur 指数的有限 N 效应。

Result: 证明了内部零点存在的充要条件是 δ(ν) 以最近零点决定的速率指数增长；内部零点阻碍自由或 s-禁闭红外描述；在巨引力子展开中，内部零点是有限 N 效应，由一个巨引力子的能量定位，并在 N=4 U(N) Schur 指数中得到验证。

Conclusion: 超对称指数的内部零点是一种可判定的、具有明确物理后果的结构特征，它既约束了红外有效理论的可能类型，又在全息对偶中对应有限 N 的巨引力子效应。

Abstract: A supersymmetric index has interior zeros ($|q|<1$) if and only if the arithmetic coefficients $δ(ν)$ underlying the supersymmetric zeta function grow exponentially at a rate set by the nearest zero. Each $δ(ν)$ follows from finitely many $q$-series coefficients, so interior zeros are detectable from the expansion alone and obstruct a free or s-confining infrared description, as we show for 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ $SU(2)$ SQCD. With a giant graviton expansion interior zeros are a finite $N$ effect, located by the energy of one giant graviton, verified for the $\mathcal{N}=4$ $U(N)$ Schur index.

</details>


### [190] [Nonlocal four-fermion theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28160)
*F. M. Belchior,J. R. Nascimento,A. Yu. Petrov,P. J. Porfirio*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了一种非局域四费米子理论，其中Dirac算符被一个完整的非局域形状因子所变形。通过引入辅助标量场，推导了平均场有效作用量和动力学质量的能隙方程。核心技术难点在于非局域形状因子是Dirac算符的矩阵函数，因此逆传播子必须在由恒等算符和$\not{\!p}$生成的闭合代数中处理。研究了两种形状因子（指数型和振荡型），并利用IR/UV匹配方法得到显式的能隙核表达式，表明双曲型形状因子增强能隙积分并降低临界耦合，而振荡型则相反。最后将结果推广至有限温度和有限密度情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的NJL/Gross-Neveu模型是局域的四费米子理论，在描述手征对称性破缺和动力学质量生成方面非常成功，但局域性导致紫外发散，需要引入截断。本文的动机在于探索一种非局域变形，用整个非局域形状因子替代局域Dirac算符，以期在保持可解性的同时，研究非局域性对能隙方程和临界耦合的影响，并为有限温度/密度推广奠定基础。

Method: 核心方法如下：(1) 将Dirac算符用非局域形状因子$f(\not{\!\partial})$变形，构建非局域四费米子理论；(2) 引入辅助标量场进行Hubbard-Stratonovich变换，推导平均场有效作用量；(3) 将逆传播子作为$\{1, \not{\!p}\}$代数中的元素处理，得到能隙核的显式表达式；(4) 选择两种代表性形状因子：$f_I=e^{-\not{\partial}/\Lambda}$（双曲型/指数衰减型）和$f_{II}=e^{-i\not{\partial}/\Lambda}$（振荡型）；(5) 采用IR/UV匹配方法，在中间尺度$M\ll\Omega\ll\Lambda$处分裂动量积分，分别在红外区和紫外区解析展开，与局域NJL/Gross-Neveu结果进行对比；(6) 通过Matsubara求和与修正的围道表示将理论推广至有限温度和有限密度。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 成功推导了非局域能隙方程的显式形式，逆传播子必须在$\{1, \not{\!p}\}$代数中处理而非标量处理；(2) 双曲型形状因子$f_I$增强了能隙积分，降低了临界耦合常数；(3) 振荡型形状因子$f_{II}$抑制了能隙积分，提高了临界耦合常数；(4) 在IR/UV匹配分析中，得到了与局域NJL/Gross-Neveu模型可比较的解析表达式；(5) 在$\Lambda\to\infty$极限下，有限温度和有限密度的非局域能隙方程正确恢复为局域热力学能隙方程。

Conclusion: 非局域形状因子的引入以可控的方式改变了手征对称性破缺的条件：双曲型（指数衰减）形状因子使动力学质量更容易生成（临界耦合降低），而振荡型形状因子则抑制质量生成（临界耦合升高）。该框架成功推广到有限温度和有限密度情形，且在$\Lambda\to\infty$极限下与局域理论一致。这一非局域方法为研究超越标准局域NJL模型的手征相变提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: In this work, we formulate and analyze a nonlocal four-fermion theory in which the usual Dirac operator is deformed by an entire nonlocal form factor. After introducing an auxiliary scalar field, we derive the mean-field effective action and the corresponding gap equation for the dynamical mass. A central technical point of the analysis is that the nonlocal form factor is a matrix function of the Dirac operator, so the inverse propagator must be treated as an element of the closed algebra generated by the identity and $\not{\!p}$ operators, rather than as a purely scalar quantity. We obtain explicit expressions for the gap kernel for two representative choices of a form factor, $f_{I}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-\not{\partial}/Λ}$ and $f_{II}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-i\not{\partial}/Λ}$. Following the IR/UV matching method used in the recent Dirac-like nonlocal spinor theory [1], the momentum integral is split at an intermediate scale $M\ll Ω\ll Λ$, expanded analytically in the infrared and ultraviolet regions, and compared with the usual local NJL/Gross-Neveu result. We show that the hyperbolic form factor enhances the gap integral and lowers the critical coupling, whereas the oscillatory form factor suppresses it and raises the critical coupling. The finite-temperature and finite-density extension is formulated through Matsubara sums and a corrected contour representation, with the local thermal gap equation recovered in the limit $Λ\to\infty$.

</details>


### [191] [Light-Front approach to $4d$ massless Higher-Spin interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28183)
*Mattia Serrani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本博士论文在光前框架下研究四维无质量高自旋相互作用，通过分析四次阶庞加莱代数的闭合性，分类了所有单导数和双导数理论，澄清了 celestial CFT 中 OPE 结合性、振幅消失、雅可比恒等式与光锥全纯约束之间的关系，并进一步研究了非全纯约束，分类了所有幺正局域高自旋理论及新的准手征理论族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究四维无质量高自旋相互作用的本质，特别是理解哪些高自旋理论在四次阶可以保持庞加莱不变性（即代数闭合），以揭示高自旋规范理论与引力理论的扩展结构及其与 celestial 全息对偶的联系。

Method: 采用光前（Light-Front）方法，通过分析庞加莱代数在四次阶的闭合条件来约束相互作用顶点。具体包括：求解光锥全纯四次约束、分类单导数和双导数理论；借助 celestial CFT 的 OPE 结合性、树级振幅的因子化、运动学代数的雅可比恒等式以及 spinor-helicity 形式来建立联系；进一步分析涉及 MHV 与反 MHV 顶点的非全纯四次约束，并纳入高导数三次顶点以寻找完整约束的非平凡解。

Result: 1. 在平坦空间中求解光锥全纯四次约束，证明存在无穷多个具有有限或无限数目场的局域相互作用高自旋理论；2. 分类了所有单导数和双导数理论，它们对应于自对偶 Yang-Mills 和自对偶引力的高自旋扩展，是 Chiral Higher-Spin Gravity 的截断；3. 阐明了 celestial CFT 的 OPE 结合性、一般运动学下树级振幅消失、"规范代数"雅可比恒等式以及光锥全纯约束之间的等价关系；4. 在非全纯约束下恢复了 Yang-Mills 理论和引力的存在性以及多引力子相互作用的非一致性；5. 发现引入高导数三次顶点后，完整四次约束存在非平凡解，分类了所有幺正局域高自旋理论，识别了新的局域准手征理论族，并确定了所有局域高自旋四点振幅。

Conclusion: 光前方法为高自旋相互作用的系统分类提供了有力工具。全纯约束给出与自对偶理论相关的一致子 sector，而非全纯约束则揭示了 Yang-Mills 与引力的特殊地位。纳入高导数顶点后，存在超出已知理论的新局域高自旋理论族，且四点振幅可通过 spinor-helicity 形式与局域因子化条件完全确定。

Abstract: This thesis studies $4d$ massless higher-spin interactions in the Light-Front approach by analysing the closure of the Poincaré algebra at quartic order. We first solve the light-cone quartic holomorphic constraint in flat space and show the existence of infinitely many interacting local higher-spin theories with either a finite or infinite number of fields. We classify all one- and two-derivative theories, corresponding to higher-spin extensions of gauge and gravitational interactions. These are consistent subsectors of higher-spin extensions of self-dual Yang--Mills and gravity, themselves truncations of Chiral Higher-Spin Gravity. We then clarify the relation between the OPE associativity in celestial CFT, the vanishing of tree-level amplitudes for generic kinematics, the Jacobi identity of the associated ''gauge algebra'' (kinematical algebra), and the light-cone holomorphic constraints. Finally, we investigate the non-holomorphic quartic constraint involving both MHV and anti-MHV vertices. We recover the existence of Yang-Mills theory and gravity, and the inconsistency of interacting multi-graviton theories. We then show that once higher-derivative cubic vertices are included, nontrivial solutions to the full quartic constraint exist. We classify all unitary local higher-spin theories, identify new families of local quasi-chiral theories, and determine all local higher-spin four-point amplitudes using the spinor-helicity formalism together with locality in the form of consistent factorisation.

</details>


### [192] [Missing Descendants in the Carrollian Conformal Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28400)
*Yu-fan Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了完整的卡罗尔共形表示，揭示了与标准共形场论的关键差异：由于 [P^0, K^0]=0，存在一条独立于平移 descendant 的 K^0 下降链。该完整表示包含平移 descendant 和 K^0 下降链，可用于描述平直全息中的大质量态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准卡罗尔共形代数中，对易关系 [P^0, K^0]=0 导致 K^0 湮灭所有由平移生成的一阶 descendant，使得标准共形族不包含 K^0 连续下降的独立 descendant 链。因此需要构建完整的卡罗尔共形表示来弥补这一缺失。

Method: 作者构造了包含 K^0 下降链及其全部平移 descendant 的完整卡罗尔共形表示，推导了相应的局域算符和轨道结构，并在 2D 和 3D 中验证了 Casimir 算符。利用全局两点 Ward 恒等式，确定了磁 non-contact 分支和电 contact 分支的运动学因子与选择定则。

Result: 完整表示包含满足 C₂ = m² = κρ − β² > 0 (2D) 和 C₂ = m² = κρ − β⃗² > 0 (3D) 的扇区。与普通共形对称性不同，卡罗尔共形对称性通常只能将关联函数确定到任意函数的程度，而非固定常数。这些扇区可能描述平直全息中的大质量态。

Conclusion: 卡罗尔共形场论具有比标准共形场论更丰富的表示结构，其关联函数具有更大的不确定性。完整表示中的大质量扇区为平直全息对偶提供了新的候选描述。

Abstract: In the Carrollian conformal algebra, the relation $[P^0,K^0]=0$ implies that $K^0$ annihilates all operators generated from a primary by temporal translation $P^0$. The standard conformal family defined by translation descendants does not contain the independent descendant chain that $K^0$ lowers successively to the primary. We construct the complete Carrollian conformal representation by including this chain together with all of its translation descendants. We derived the corresponding local operators, orbit structures, and checked their Casimirs in 2D and 3D. For each orbit configuration, the global two-point Ward identities fix the kinematic factors and selection rules for both magnetic non-contact branches and electric contact branches. Unlike ordinary conformal symmetry, Carrollian conformal symmetry generally determines the correlators only up to arbitrary functions of Carrollian invariants rather than constants. The complete representation contains sectors with $\mathcal{C}_2=m^2=κρ-β^2>0$ for 2D and $\mathcal{C}_2=m^2=κρ-\vecβ^{\,2}>0$ for 3D, which may describe massive states in flat holography.

</details>


### [193] [Covariant variation and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28420)
*Wen-Bin Liu,Jiang Long*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文定义了一种协变变分算子，通过将李导数和度量变分相结合，保留了度量、缩并和霍奇对偶性，但其对易子因异常而不闭合。作者推导了其代数和几何性质，并与Kosmann导数比较，进而构造了总协变变分及一系列扩展算子。该框架应用于超转动下的异常，在四维零超曲面产生电磁螺度流，并推广到任意时空维度的张量场，特别关注d=2p+2中的p-形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于统一处理具有时空结构和洛伦兹结构的场的变分问题。传统的李导数不保持洛伦兹结构，而Kosmann导数虽保持洛伦兹结构但不保持缩并等操作。作者希望构造一种能同时保持度量、缩并和霍奇对偶性的协变变分算子，并探讨其对易子的异常现象及其物理含义。

Method: 通过将张量场的李导数与度量变分相结合，定义了一个新的协变变分算子。作者推导了该算子的代数性质（包括对易子的异常结构）和几何性质，并与Kosmann导数进行了系统比较。进一步将该协变变分与Kosmann导数结合，构造了适用于同时具有时空和洛伦兹结构场的总协变变分，并引入了一族包含仿射联络、李导数和协变变分的扩展算子。应用方面，在四维超转动背景下分析协变变分异常，并推广到d=2p+2维中的p-形式。

Result: 该协变变分算子成功保持度量、缩并和霍奇对偶性，但其对易子存在异常，无法闭合。从超转动下协变变分的异常中，在四维零超曲面上自然涌现出电磁螺度流。此外，该框架被成功推广到任意时空维度的张量场，特别是d=2p+2维中的p-形式情况。

Conclusion: 作者提出的协变变分算子为统一处理时空和洛伦兹结构的场变分提供了新工具。其异常结构并非缺陷，而是具有物理意义——在四维情形下直接关联于电磁螺度流。该框架可系统推广到任意维度，为引力理论和规范场论中的对称性与异常研究开辟了新视角。

Abstract: We define a covariant variation of tensor fields by combining its Lie derivative with the metric variation. This operator preserves the metric, contractions, and Hodge duality, but its commutator is not closed due to an anomaly. We derive its algebraic and geometric properties, and compare it with the Kosmann derivative. Combining the covariant variation with Kosmann derivative gives total covariant variation for the fields with both spacetime and Lorentz structure, all of which belong to the metric Lie derivative. Moreover, we introduce families of extended operators which contain the affine connection, Lie derivative, and covariant variation. From the anomaly of the covariant variation along the superrotation, an electromagnetic helicity flux appears at null hypersurfaces in four dimensions. We also apply the covariant variation and its anomaly to tensor fields in arbitrary spacetime dimensions, and especially focus on the $p$-forms in $d=2p+2$ dimensions.

</details>


### [194] [Hayden--Preskill recovery at finite temperature on a quantum processor: dynamics and initial state from the SYK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28486)
*Jeongho Bang,Moongul Byun,Kyoungho Cho,Keun-Young Kim,Hyeonsoo Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Hayden-Preskill信息恢复协议，通过SWAP门使加扰器与初态相关联，并考虑有限温度情形。研究表明信息可成功恢复，且后选择概率与条件保真度均与温度成正比。在IBM超导处理器上使用N=8 Majorana费米子的二元稀疏SYK哈密顿量进行了实验验证，并采用基于SWAP的误差缓解方案提升了恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Hayden-Preskill恢复协议中，注入后的加扰器与初态无关。作者旨在探索两者相关联时的信息恢复动力学，以及有限温度对恢复过程的影响，从而更全面地理解量子信息加扰与恢复的物理机制。

Method: (1) 理论扩展：引入SWAP门使加扰器与初态关联，推广至有限温度；(2) 解析推导：在均匀算符扩散假设下导出后选择概率和条件保真度的长时间渐近估计；(3) 数值模拟：验证解析结果；(4) 实验实现：在IBM超导量子处理器上，使用N=8 Majorana费米子的二元稀疏SYK哈密顿量实现协议，并应用基于SWAP的误差缓解方案。

Result: 后选择概率和条件保真度均正比于温度，反映了低温下初态纠缠减少；均匀算符扩散假设下的长时间解析估计与数值结果吻合良好，表明强加扰对成功恢复至关重要；IBM超导处理器上的实验数据保留了定性的恢复动力学特征；SWAP误差缓解方案同时改善了后选择概率和条件保真度。

Conclusion: 加扰器与初态关联的Hayden-Preskill协议在有限温度下仍可实现信息恢复，强加扰是关键因素。该协议在含噪量子硬件上具有可行性，且SWAP误差缓解可有效提升性能。

Abstract: In the original Hayden--Preskill recovery, the post-injection scrambler and initial state are {\it not related}. We extend this setup in two ways: by using a SWAP gate so that the scrambler and initial state are {\it related}, and by considering recovery at {\it finite} temperature. For this modified protocol, we show that the information is successfully recovered in the sense that the postselection probability is non-negligible and the conditional fidelity is large. We find that both the postselection probability and the conditional fidelity are proportional to temperature, reflecting the reduced entanglement of the initial state at lower temperatures. We also derive their late-time analytic estimates under the assumption of uniform operator spreading and show that they agree well with the numerical results. This demonstrates that strong scrambling is important for successful information recovery. Implementing the protocol on an IBM superconducting processor using a binary sparse SYK Hamiltonian with $N = 8$ Majoranas, we observe that the data retain the qualitative recovery dynamics and that a SWAP-based error-mitigation scheme improves both the postselection probability and the conditional fidelity.

</details>


### [195] [Soft charges and zero modes at null boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28543)
*Dušan Đorđ ević,Olivera Miskovic,Antonia Montecinos,Tatjana Vukašinac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了零边界上的渐近对称性，揭示了一个常被忽视的全局零模模糊性。该零模修正了场论在零边界上的正则结构，导致一种新的准局域边界可观测量，并对守恒荷代数产生重要影响。这一机制普遍适用于零无穷远和有限距离（如黑洞视界）的零边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零边界的渐近对称性通常与残余规范变换相关联，但零边界还存在一个特征性的全局零模模糊性，其物理后果尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究该零模如何影响零边界上场论的正则结构及荷代数。

Method: 通过分析零边界上场论的正则形式，作者考察了全局零模如何修正标准的辛结构和守恒荷。重点在于推导该零模所诱导的新准局域边界可观测量，并计算其对荷代数的具体修正，同时论证该机制在零无穷远和有限距离零边界（如黑洞视界）上的普适性。

Result: 研究表明，全局零模导致了一个新的准局域边界可观测量，该可观测量修正了原有的守恒荷代数结构。这一效应普遍存在于各类零边界，包括渐近零无穷远和有限距离的黑洞视界。

Conclusion: 零边界的全局零模模糊性具有重要的物理意义：它丰富了边界正则结构，引入新的准局域可观测量，并修正荷代数。这为理解场论的软扇区、黑洞视界物理以及引力/规范全息对偶中的边界对称性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Asymptotic symmetries at null boundaries provide a powerful window into the soft sector of field theories, revealing conserved charges and infinite-dimensional symmetry algebras beyond the standard bulk dynamics. While these structures are usually associated with residual gauge transformations, null boundaries also exhibit a characteristic global zero-mode ambiguity, whose physical consequences are less explored. We discuss how this zero mode modifies the canonical structure of field theories at null boundaries. In particular, we show that it gives rise to a new quasilocal edge observable, with consequences to the charge algebra. It can arise on any kind of null boundaries, both at the null infinity and the finite distance such as a black hole horizon.

</details>


### [196] [Learning to Trace Seiberg Dualities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28628)
*Jonathan J. Heckman,Shani Meynet,Alessandro Mininno,Gary Shiu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习方法研究超对称规范理论中的Seiberg对偶性问题，将箭图突变识别转化为网络学习任务。研究发现对于节点数约10个的箭图，Transformer和多层感知机网络架构优于确定性算法，结合寻路算法可进一步提升搜索效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶性在物理系统中至关重要，但即使规则已知，判定两个系统是否对偶在计算上往往极具挑战性。作者旨在利用机器学习方法高效判定Seiberg对偶性（即箭图突变），并探索不同网络架构如何学习这一任务。

Method: 将Seiberg对偶性对应的箭图突变问题转化为机器学习任务。采用Transformer和多层感知机（MLP）等不同网络架构进行训练与比较，并引入成熟的寻路算法（pathfinder algorithms）作为网络补充，以提升搜索策略的效率与准确度。

Result: 在箭图节点数约为10的中等规模设定下，Transformer和MLP网络架构表现优于确定性算法；结合寻路算法后，搜索效率和准确度均获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法可有效用于判定Seiberg对偶性，且此类问题有望成为前沿AI模型在理论物理领域的有用基准测试。

Abstract: Dualities play an important role in establishing both microscopic and emergent phenomena in a wide range of physical systems. In practice, though, it can often be computationally challenging to establish when two systems are dual, even when all of the "rules of the game" are well-known. Said differently, when confronted with two systems, how can one efficiently establish that they are in fact dual? In this paper we use machine learning methods to address this question for Seiberg dualities of supersymmetric quiver gauge theories. Mathematically, this involves establishing mutations of quivers, which is in turn a variation on the theme of "learning to unknot". On the one hand, this leads us to a practical tool for establishing the computational complexity of different dualities. On the other hand, it also allows us to study how different network architectures learn how to trace Seiberg dualities. We find that for quivers with a modest number of quiver nodes (of order $10$), different network architectures consisting of transformers and multi-layer perceptrons tend to outperform deterministic algorithms. Supplementing the network by well-established pathfinder algorithms (essentially "Google Maps for quivers") leads to an additional improvement in the efficiency and accuracy of the search strategy. We anticipate that this class of questions can serve as a useful benchmark for frontier AI models applied to theoretical physics.

</details>

### [197] [Thermal Breaking of the I-Love Universality for Hot White Dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27873)
*Jinyi Lv,Jing-Yi Wu,Hongji Chen,Kexin Jia,Weihan Sun,Kilar Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用MESA恒星演化代码构建不同温度下的白矮星模型，通过Clairaut-Radau方程定量分析内部等密度面偏心率的径向变化，揭示了温度通过破坏等密度面自相似性从而导致I-Love普适关系偏离的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I-Love-Q普适关系在引力波天文学中有重要应用，但热效应会破坏低质量白矮星中的这些关系。现有研究缺乏对温度如何破坏等密度面自相似性这一根本机制的定量理解，需要揭示普适关系失效的物理本质及其适用条件。

Method: 使用MESA恒星演化代码构建0.15 M☉氦核和0.6 M☉碳氧核白矮星在不同温度下的真实模型，采用Clairaut-Radau方程定量提取内部等密度面偏心率的径向变化，并与零温Chandrasekhar模型进行对比分析。

Result: 数值结果表明，较高的中心温度会放大等密度面偏心率的径向变化，导致I-Love关系偏离零温Chandrasekhar模型；随后的冷却过程能使这些关系恢复，证实了温度诱导的普适关系偏离根本来源于等密度面自相似性的丧失。

Conclusion: 温度引起的I-Love-Q普适关系偏离的物理根源在于等密度面自相似性的破坏。该发现为致密天体普适关系的适用条件提供了关键理论约束，对引力波天文学中利用这些关系进行参数推断具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: The universal I-Love-Q relations for compact stars have significant applications in gravitational-wave astronomy, but thermal effects can break these relations in low-mass white dwarfs. In this work, we employ the stellar evolution code MESA to construct realistic models of $0.15 \, M_{\odot}$ helium-core and $0.6 \, M_{\odot}$ carbon-oxygen core white dwarfs at various temperatures. By utilizing the Clairaut-Radau equation, we quantitatively extract the radial variation of the eccentricity of internal isodensity surfaces. Our numerical results demonstrate that higher central temperatures amplify the eccentricity variation, causing the I-Love relations to deviate from the zero-temperature Chandrasekhar model, whereas subsequent cooling restores them. This confirms that the temperature-induced violation of the universal relations is fundamentally driven by the loss of self-similarity in isodensity surfaces, providing key insights into the applicability conditions of I-Love-Q relations in compact objects.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Design principles for energy dissipation in viscoelastic network metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27525)
*Niranjan Sarpangala,Sean Fancher,Prashant K. Purohit,Eleni Katifori*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图拉普拉斯算子的高效谱框架，用于精确求解粘弹性桁架网络的连续体动力学，并发现梯度优化可得到由材料本征衰减长度主导的非平凡耗散架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型无序桁架网络中识别最优耗散架构对减振和冲击防护至关重要，但传统有限元方法计算代价过高，亟需一种高效的计算框架。

Method: 开发了基于图拉普拉斯算子的谱方法框架，精确保留每根杆的完整连续体动力学，问题规模仅与节点数相关（而非单元离散点数）；利用该框架研究截面面积重分配对能量耗散的影响，并采用梯度优化寻找最优架构。

Result: 随机重分配通常降低耗散（相对均匀基线）；梯度优化得到的架构由基体材料的本征衰减长度主导；在接近全局共振模式的驱动频率下，最优质量分布从驱动源以衰减长度尺度衰减，且小衰减长度下最优架构与边界条件无关。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于耗散长度尺度的设计原则在更复杂无序架构中的研究奠定了基础，并提供了可大规模探索此类结构的高效计算框架。

Abstract: Mechanical energy dissipation in networked materials is relevant for applications from vibration isolation to impact protection, yet identifying optimal dissipative architectures in large disordered truss networks is computationally prohibitive with conventional finite element methods. We develop an efficient graph Laplacian-based spectral framework for viscoelastic truss networks, in which the full continuum dynamics of each rod are retained exactly and the problem size scales with the number of joints rather than element-level discretization points. Using this framework, we investigate how redistributing cross-sectional areas within a network (without changing material composition) controls energy dissipation. We find that random redistribution typically reduces dissipation relative to a uniform baseline, while gradient-based optimization yields nontrivial architectures whose form is governed by the intrinsic attenuation length of the base material. Focusing on driving frequencies near a global resonant mode of the network, we show that the optimal mass distribution decays from the source (driven joint) with the attenuation length scale, and at small attenuation lengths the optimal architecture is independent of the boundary conditions. These results motivate future studies of dissipation length scale based design principles on more complex disordered architectures and provide an efficient computational framework for exploring such structures at scale.

</details>


### [199] [Partial vision leads to an unexpected emergent collective behavior in active aligning particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27819)
*Raúl Molina-Prados Lallena,José Martín-Roca,Chantal Valeriani*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文将经典Vicsek模型推广至具有两个非重叠角视锥的有限感知场景，系统研究了视锥孔径和前后取向对系统非平衡相行为的影响，揭示了前后视觉不对称性引发的非互易相互作用如何塑造全新的空间结构，并进一步考察了体积排斥效应的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Vicsek模型假设各向同性相互作用场，而许多生物系统（如动物群体）的个体感知具有方向性和盲区。目前缺乏对有限感知、非对称视觉场如何影响活性物质集体动力学和相行为的系统理解。

Method: 构建了一个广义Vicsek模型，引入两个非重叠的角视锥，通过独立调节视锥孔径面积（α）和前后取向（β）来模拟有限感知和视觉不对称性。系统研究非平衡相行为，包括有序-无序转变、密度涨落和空间结构。进一步引入短程体积排斥以检验 steric 相互作用的影响。

Result: (1) 限制侧向视觉面积会破坏全局有序，移动临界噪声并诱导高密度行波带；(2) 打破前后对称性引入非互易相互作用，前向偏好视觉通过"跟随领导者"机制驱动强聚集，而后向偏好视觉则稳定出奇均匀的 flocking 态，密度涨落被显著抑制；(3) 这些新型紧密聚集相的结构完整性对 steric 相互作用高度敏感。

Conclusion: 非互易感知、空间各向异性和物理约束三者在活性物质中相互交织，共同决定了系统的集体动力学和空间结构。该工作为理解生物群体中有限感知与不对称信息流的作用提供了新的理论见解。

Abstract: The Vicsek Model represents a paradigmatic framework for understanding the collective motion of active aligning particles, traditionally assuming isotropic interaction fields. Inspired by biological systems characterized by limited perception and blind spots, we propose a generalized Vicsek model featuring two distinct, non-overlapping angular vision cones. We systematically investigate the non-equilibrium phase behavior of this system by tuning the aperture area (α) and the front-back orientation (\b{eta}) of the cones. Our results reveal that restricting the lateral vision area destabilizes global order, shifts the critical noise, and induces highly dense traveling bands. Furthermore, breaking the front-back symmetry introduces non-reciprocal interactions that profoundly alter the emergent spatial structures: forward-biased vision drives strong clustering through "follow the leader" alignment, whereas backward-biased alignment stabilizes an exceptionally homogeneous flocking state with suppressed density fluctuations. Finally, we incorporate short-range volume exclusion, demonstrating that the structural integrity of these novel tightly-clustered phases is highly sensitive to steric interactions. Our work provides new insights into the interplay between non-reciprocal perception, spatial anisotropy, and physical constraints in active matter.

</details>


### [200] [Phase transitions and microphases in elastomers. I. Emergence of stable domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27516)
*Manu Mannattil,Haim Diamant,David Andelman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文建立了弹性体微相分离的非局部热力学-弹性耦合理论。研究表明，溶胀弹性体中的微相分离源于弹性作用与热力学相互作用之间的特征长度尺度失配，而体积守恒导致的非局部耦合是核心机制。该理论成功重现了各向同性溶胀弹性体中相变温度和畴尺寸随刚度变化的实验观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性在物理系统的相分离中起关键调控作用，近期实验表明可利用弹性效应控制溶胀弹性体中的微相分离。然而，其背后的理论机制尚不清晰，需要从经典弹性理论出发，为微相分离现象提供一个统一的力学解释框架。

Method: 基于经典弹性理论，引入由体积守恒引起的非局部热力学-弹性耦合机制。该耦合将弹性变形与热力学相分离通过非局部项联系起来，从而自然导出与弹性刚度相关的特征长度尺度，解释微相畴的形成。

Result: 理论成功重现了各向同性溶胀弹性体中两个关键实验现象：(1) 相变温度对弹性体刚度的依赖性；(2) 微相畴尺寸对弹性体刚度的依赖性。两者均与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 微相分离可通过经典弹性理论中的非局部热力学-弹性耦合加以解释，体积守恒是产生该耦合的物理根源。本文为理解弹性体微相分离奠定了理论基础，后续姊妹篇将进一步探讨各向异性溶胀和非均匀弹性模量的效应。

Abstract: Elasticity often plays a key role in regulating phase separation in physical systems. Recent experiments have shown that elastic effects can be used to control microphase separation in swollen elastomers. Here, microphase separation arises from a mismatch between the characteristic length scales of elastic and thermodynamic interactions. In this first part of a two-part paper, we show that microphase formation in elastomers can be explained using conventional theories of elasticity through a nonlocal thermodynamic-elastic coupling arising from volume conservation. Our theory reproduces the observed dependence of phase transition temperature and domain size on elastomer stiffness in isotropically swollen elastomers. In the companion paper, we investigate the effects of anisotropic swelling and inhomogeneous elastic moduli.

</details>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [On two differing geometric descriptions of the passage from microscopy to macroscopy in Markov diffusion theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28578)
*Dalton A R Sakthivadivel*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文在闭黎曼流形上构造了一个中心数学对象，用于调和扩散过程中微观-介观-宏观层次描述之间的两种不同连续性算子。将光滑正概率密度空间视为Fréchet流形，通过其切空间的不同Hilbert完备化，对Markov分析中的层次结构给出了部分统一化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机过程如何导出抛物型偏微分方程以及相应的梯度泛函不等式，涉及粒子、场、经验测度、光滑密度律及其泛函等多个描述层次。这些层次之间的转换依赖于连续性算子如何将粒子速度转化为测度演化。本文的动机在于构造一个中介性的数学对象，以协调两种不同的描述方式，从而实现该层次结构的部分统一化。

Method: 将闭黎曼流形上的光滑正概率密度空间处理为Fréchet流形，其完全连续余切空间由非常数分布构成，正则余切空间由足够正则的非常数函数构成。从底流形微分同胚的无穷小提升出发，对该密度空间的两种不同Hilbert完备化进行分析，涵盖了一类广泛的 Markov 分析相关对象。

Result: 成功构造了一个中心对象，能够中介扩散理论中两种不同的描述方式。两种Hilbert完备化能够涵盖 Markov 分析中微观-介观-宏观层次的大量相关对象，实现了该层次结构的部分统一化。

Conclusion: 通过将密度空间几何化并引入不同的Hilbert完备化，可以为扩散过程的微观-介观-宏观层次提供一种统一化的数学语言，这对 Markov 分析及相关领域具有理论意义。

Abstract: In various disparate settings one studies how random processes give way to parabolic partial differential equations and in turn to functional inequalities involving gradients and variational calculus. Particles subject to gradient forces, the fields which move them, the empirical measures which they form, the smooth laws which those measures approximate, and differentiable functionals of these laws constitute a hierarchy of descriptions of diffusion. Intervening on this hierarchy is a choice of how a conintuity operator converts particle velocity to the evolution of measures. Here a central object is constructed mediating two different such descriptions. The space of smooth positive probability densities on a closed Riemannian manifold is treated as a Fréchet manifold whose full continuous cotangent space consists of nonconstant distributions and whose regular cotangent space consists of sufficiently regular nonconstant functions. Beginning from the operator on this space arising as the infinitesimal lift of diffeomorphisms of the base manifold, two different Hilbert completions of this space of densities account for a wide class of objects relevant, leading to a partial universalisation of the microscopic--mesoscopic--macroscopic hierarchy in Markov analysis.

</details>

### [202] [Data Field Theory: Theory and Applications of the Functional Renormalization Group for Signal Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27236)
*Riccardo Finotello,Vincent Lahoche,Dine Ousmane Samary,Parham Radpay*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 本文综述了高维数据中信号检测的重整化群框架，特别针对信号可能具有广延秩且不作为孤立尖峰从噪声体中分离的情形。该框架通过在准连续谱区追踪谱变形，提供了一种无需依赖尖峰分离的信号判据，其核心是检验有效场论高斯不动点的稳定性，信号引起的标度依赖性则表现为维度相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准方法（如BBP阈值）要求特征值必须干净地从噪声体中分离出来，但在信号具有广延秩、嵌入准连续谱区的情形下失效。因此需要一种不依赖尖峰分离、能够直接追踪谱变形并在该区域提供一致检测下限的新框架。

Method: 采用重整化群框架，将谱尾集体自由度建模为有效场论，通过检验该理论高斯不动点的稳定性来判别信号存在与否。信号引起正则维度的标度依赖性，表现为维度相变，从而给出检测下限。

Result: 该框架在准连续谱区一致地给出了信号检测的下限，识别出信号通过破坏高斯不动点稳定性而显现，这一机制超越了传统BBP尖峰分离范式的适用范围。

Conclusion: 重整化群方法为高维信号检测提供了概念简洁且更普适的准则，尤其适用于信号嵌入随机矩阵普适类附近准连续谱区的场景，其核心物理图像是维度相变与不动点稳定性分析。

Abstract: We review the renormalization group framework for signal detection in high-dimensional data, tailored to the regime where the signal may be of extensive rank and does not separate from the noise bulk as isolated spikes. The framework provides a conceptually simple criterion for distinguishing signal from noise within a quasi-continuous spectral region near a random-matrix universality class. This scenario lies beyond the reach of standard methods such as the Baik-Ben Arous-Péché threshold, which requires eigenvalues to be cleanly separated from the bulk. The renormalization group approach, by contrast, directly tracks spectral deformations and consistently yields a lower limit of detection without relying on spike separation. We review results that identify the presence of a signal by testing the stability of the Gaussian fixed point of an effective field theory for the collective behaviour of the degrees of freedom in the spectral tail, where the signal resides. We also discuss how the scale dependence of the canonical dimension, induced by the signal, manifests as a dimensional phase transition.

</details>

### [203] [Information-driven stepping in dimeric transport motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27495)
*Antonio Patrón Castro,David A. Sivak*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个二聚体运输马达的布朗运动理论模型，两个马达头以位置依赖的方式周期性切换迁移率，使得每次仅有一个头移动。模型揭示该马达作为纯粹的信息引擎运作，通过麦克斯韦妖式的信息转导实现定向运动，成功复现了实验观测的平均速度和失速力，并通过粗粒化得到了四种驻留时间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二聚体分子马达如何通过消耗化学自由能实现定向运动的物理机制，特别是揭示其内部能量与信息流动的热力学本质，以及解释实验中观测到的失速力和非马尔可夫动力学特征。

Method: 构建双头布朗运动模型，引入位置依赖的迁移率周期性切换机制（每次仅一个头移动）；在稳态下表征马达的平均速度、能量流和信息流；将模型粗粒化为单一机械自由度的二阶非马尔可夫动力学，推导四种驻留时间分布。

Result: （1）马达作为纯信息引擎运行，组件间仅转导信息而无能量交换；（2）局部化切换条件下，得到热力学一致的平均速度表达式，复现实验行为；（3）模型自然捕捉到紧密耦合马达的失速力特征；（4）粗粒化后导出可直接在单分子实验中观测的四种驻留时间分布。

Conclusion: 信息转导（通过隐式的麦克斯韦妖机制）可能是分子马达卓越性能的基础，该理论框架为理解分子马达的动力学和热力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: Dimeric transport motors are nanoscale protein complexes that move along cytoskeletal filaments. Here we introduce a theoretical model for their stepping dynamics, in which the two motor heads undergo Brownian motion with mobilities periodically switching in a position-dependent manner so that only one head moves at a time. Through consumption of chemical free energy, this mechanism produces directed motion. We characterize at steady state the motor's mean velocity and its energy and information flows. The motor operates as a pure information engine, where only information is transduced between its components, without energy exchange. For localized switching, the model yields a thermodynamically consistent expression for the mean motor velocity that reproduces experimentally observed behavior, and captures the stall force characteristic of tightly coupled motors. Finally, coarse-graining the model to a single mechanical degree of freedom produces a second-order non-Markovian dynamics, from which we compute the four distinct dwell-time distributions that can be directly observed in single-molecule experiments. Our findings highlight how information transduction, via implicit operation as a Maxwell demon, may underlie the remarkable performance of these molecular motors.

</details>
