### [204] [Chaos in reason: How chain-of-thought LLMs can look for an answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27805)
*Gregorio Jaca,Kristóf Benedek,János Török*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 该论文将非线性动力学和混沌理论应用于大语言模型（LLM），揭示LLM展现出混沌系统的典型特征，包括对初始条件的敏感依赖性、隐藏状态空间中的分形结构，并提出注意力机制的非线性耦合是驱动混沌行为的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在各种任务上表现优异，但其内部动力学机制仍然不清楚。研究者希望借助非线性动力学和混沌理论的工具，深入理解LLM内部的信息处理动态过程。

Method: 1. 轨迹分析：分析文本和隐藏状态轨迹，使用多种距离度量检测对初始条件的敏感性及邻近轨迹的间歇性跳跃式发散；2. 雅可比分析：对Transformer各子模块（自注意力、前馈网络、归一化层、残差连接）进行精确的雅可比矩阵分析，量化各组件对扰动的扩张与抑制效应；3. 递归图分析：比较LLM与Lorenz吸引子等典型混沌系统的结构相似性；4. 维度分析：揭示隐藏状态空间中的分形结构，尤其在最后几层表现显著。

Result: 1. LLM展现出混沌的标志性特征：对初始条件敏感、邻近轨迹呈间歇性跳跃式发散且演化有界，不同距离度量结果一致；2. 自注意力和前馈网络扩张并传播扰动，而层归一化和残差连接则抑制扩张、促进稳定；3. 递归图显示LLM与Lorenz吸引子等混沌系统存在结构相似性；4. 隐藏状态空间中存在分形结构，尤其在模型的最后几层最为显著。

Conclusion: LLM的内部动力学具有混沌本质，注意力机制引入的非线性耦合是驱动混沌行为的关键。这一发现为理解LLM的工作机制提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, yet their internal dynamics remain poorly understood. In this work, we apply the tools of nonlinear dynamics and chaos theory to LLMs. By analyzing both text and hidden state trajectories, we demonstrate that LLMs exhibit hallmark signatures of chaos, including strong sensitivity to initial conditions, manifested as intermittent, jump-like divergence of nearby trajectories combined with bounded evolution, with consistent results across different distance metrics. An exact Jacobian analysis of the Transformer's sub-blocks shows that self-attention and the feed-forward network expand and propagate perturbations, while normalization and residual connections counteract this expansion and promote stability. Recurrence plots show structural similarities between LLMs and canonical chaotic systems such as the Lorenz attractor, while dimension analysis reveals fractal structures in the hidden state space, particularly pronounced in the last layers. We propose that the nonlinear coupling induced by attention mechanisms plays a key role in driving this chaotic behavior.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [The Conformal Origin of the Lepton Flavor Mixing Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19454)
*Cora Aigle,Alex Jourjine*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用味自旋理论形式，以一种新颖方式重新推导了TM1轻子味混合因子分解，发现了一个此前未报道的涉及U_PMNS第一行元素的近恒等式。该混合矩阵是无质量标准模型在电弱相变前共形对称性的直接结果。作者还推导出CP破坏相位与三个混合角之间的关系式，并建议TM1分解应采用(19/12)π作为CP破坏相位值，从而使TM1的四个参数均可用根式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索TM1轻子味混合因子分解的新推导方式，揭示其背后的共形对称性起源，并寻求用根式表达所有混合参数，以助于在味自旋理论框架中分离经典值与量子修正，进而澄清质量产生机制。

Method: 采用味自旋理论（flavor spin theory）形式体系，利用一个此前未报道的涉及PMNS矩阵第一行元素的近恒等式，重新推导TM1轻子味混合因子分解；基于电弱相变前无质量标准模型的共形对称性论证混合矩阵的必然性；推导CP破坏相位与三个混合角的关系式，并结合实验数据拟合确定CP相位的最佳取值。

Result: 1) 发现了一个涉及U_PMNS第一行元素的新近恒等式；2) 证明TM1混合矩阵是无质量SM在EW相变前共形对称性的直接推论；3) 导出了CP破坏相位δ与三个混合角(θ12, θ23, θ13)的关系式；4) 结合实验数据建议TM1分解采用δ = (19/12)π ≈ 285°，使四个参数均可用根式精确表达。

Conclusion: TM1混合矩阵源于电弱相变前标准模型的共形对称性，CP破坏相位取(19/12)π时所有TM1参数可用根式表达，这有助于在味自旋理论中将混合参数的经典值与其量子修正分离开，从而为理解质量产生机制提供新的线索。

Abstract: Using the formalism of the flavor spin theory, we re-derive the TM1 lepton flavor mixing factor decomposition in a novel way, using a previously unreported near identity involving the elements of the first row of U_PMNS. The resulting mixing matrix is a direct consequence of the conformal symmetry of the massless SM before the EW phase transition. In addition to the previously reported results, we derive a relation between the CP violating phase and the three mixing angles. Application of the relation to the experimental data data suggests the TM1 decomposition should use (19/12)Pi as the value for the CP violating phase. With this assumption all four parameters of TM1 may be expressed in radicals, which might help in the separation of the classical values of the mixing parameters from their quantum corrections in the framework of the flavor spin theories. This in turn could help in clarification of the mass generation mechanism.

</details>


### [206] [A Material Frame: Hard Recoils from Slow Force Carriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27253)
*Francesco Serra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过使光子的纵向分量以极慢速度传播来修改标准模型，破坏了规范不变性和洛伦兹对称性。该理论在c_L→0极限下可恢复标准规范理论，但实验约束极其严格：带电粒子通过切伦科夫辐射慢模产生硬反冲，暗物质探测器给出的约束为c_L ≲ 10⁻⁶⁰，比传统光速差异限制强数十个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在标准模型框架内修改光子纵向分量使其物理化、低速传播的可能性，同时不引入新场或新能标，并研究由此引发的实验唯象学后果。

Method: 通过破坏规范不变性和洛伦兹对称性，使光子的纵向分量成为物理自由度并以亚光速c_L≪1传播；引入伽利略高阶形式对称性保护光子质量；分析带电粒子切伦科夫辐射慢模的动力学，计算反冲率和动量转移，并结合暗物质探测器的keV级核反冲灵敏度导出对c_L的限制。

Result: 理论在c_L→0极限下平滑恢复到标准规范理论预言。实验约束表明：带电粒子通过切伦科夫辐射发射慢模，经历相对于优越参考系的硬反冲；反冲率随c_L降低而减小，但单次动量转移量级与粒子动量相当；暗物质探测器给出c_L ≲ 10⁻⁶⁰的量级限制。在此极端数值下，c_L更适合被理解为反冲率因子而非物理速度，慢模在空间中几乎固定，使优越参考系成为可吸收带电粒子动量的实质介质。

Conclusion: 该修改方案理论自洽，但受到极其严峻的实验约束（c_L ≲ 10⁻⁶⁰），远超传统光子速度差异限制。慢模实质上将优越参考系物化为一种动量吸收介质，这为洛伦兹破缺理论提供了新的唯象学视角和实验检验通道。

Abstract: We modify the Standard Model by making the longitudinal component of the photon physical, propagating with small speed $c_L \ll 1$. This modification breaks gauge invariance and Lorentz symmetry, defining a preferred reference frame. No new fields or scales are introduced, and the photon mass is protected by a Galilean higher-form symmetry. The theory has a smooth $c_L\to0$ limit: the new modes decouple from standard matter and ordinary gauge theory predictions are recovered. Experimental constraints are severe. Charged particles Cherenkov-emit these slow modes, experiencing hard recoils against the preferred frame. The recoil rate decreases with $c_L$, but the momentum transfer remains of the order of the particle's momentum. Dark matter detectors, sensitive to keV-scale nuclear recoils, imply an order-of-magnitude bound $c_L \lesssim 10^{-60}$, tens of orders of magnitude stronger than speed-difference bounds. At such values, $c_L$ is better understood as a recoil-rate factor rather than a physically relevant speed. The slow modes, practically fixed in space, make the reference frame a material medium capable of absorbing momentum from charged particles.

</details>


### [207] [Suppressed Quantum Effects of Weakly Coupled Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27313)
*Yunjia Bao,Dhong Yeon Cheong,Nicholas L. Rodd,Joey Takach,Lian-Tao Wang,Kevin Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了两项阻碍探测弱耦合波（如轴子暗物质和引力波）非经典量子效应的根本性障碍：一是探测器无法分辨基本模式而只能耦合粗粒化"有效模式"，导致非经典效应被洗刷；二是所有非经典效应都受到弱耦合的额外幂次压制。作者证明这一结论的普遍性，并以轴子腔晕探测器为例说明，同时指出该结论排除了通过引力波观测来确立引力量子化的提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密实验越来越多地瞄准弱耦合波（如轴子暗物质、引力波），这些波通常被描述为经典场，但理论上可存在于无经典对应的量子态中。学界存在利用此类实验探测非经典效应的期望，甚至提出通过引力波观测来验证引力量子化。作者旨在检验这些方案是否切实可行。

Method: 作者采用一般性理论分析，证明了两项与模式占据数无关的阻碍：（1）真实探测器因有限分辨率，耦合的是场的粗粒化"有效模式"而非基本模式，导致非经典效应被平均化洗刷；（2）所有非经典效应（包括正交分量统计、粒子数统计、纠缠和退相干）受到弱耦合参数的额外幂次压制。进一步论证，虽然原则上可用高度压缩的探测器态等量子资源克服压制，但所需参数远超现有实验能力。最后以轴子腔晕探测器作为显式案例进行了验证。

Result: 证明了两项普遍性的严重阻碍：有效模式粗粒化导致非经典效应被洗刷；弱耦合导致非经典效应受额外幂次压制。以轴子腔晕探测器为例，克服压制所需的量子资源参数远超当前实验能力。该结论适用于大量超轻暗物质搜索实验，并排除了通过引力波观测确立引力量子化的现有提议。

Conclusion: 在弱耦合条件下，探测场的非经典量子效应面临原理性障碍，现有及可预见的实验技术均无法克服。这意味着当前通过引力波或轴子暗物质实验来验证量子本质的提议不可行，研究重心应重新审视。

Abstract: Precision experiments increasingly target weakly coupled waves, including axion dark matter and gravitational radiation. Such waves are commonly described as classical fields, yet they could exist in quantum states with no classical counterpart. We exhibit two severe obstructions to detecting nonclassical effects, both independent of the mode occupancy. First, realistic detectors cannot resolve the fundamental modes of a field; instead they couple to coarse-grained "effective" modes, which often washes out nonclassical effects. Second, all nonclassical effects are suppressed by extra powers of the weak coupling, making them much harder to detect than the waves themselves. We prove this in general, and explicitly show how the suppression arises for quadrature and number statistics, entanglement, and decoherence. The suppression can in principle be overcome given suitable quantum resources, such as highly squeezed detector states, but the required parameters are far beyond current experimental capabilities. We use the axion cavity haloscope as an explicit example, although our conclusions apply to many ultralight dark matter searches, and rule out proposals to establish the quantization of gravity from observations of gravitational waves.

</details>


### [208] [The Dark Dimension meets the Axiverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27314)
*Kevin Langhoff,Maria Ramos,Mario Reig*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将暗维度场景与轴子宇宙（axiverse）结合带来的宇宙学效应。研究表明，除引力子KK塔外，闭弦轴子的KK激发态也可在暗维度中传播，既可通过冻结产生（freeze-in）机制提供全部暗物质，又能通过多塔之间的能量碎片化（fragmentation）抑制衰变信号，从而规避对暗维度暗物质模型最严格的宇宙学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最简单的暗维度暗物质模型面临严峻的宇宙学约束，作者试图通过引入轴子宇宙——即大量闭弦轴子的KK激发塔——来同时解释暗物质起源并自然规避这些约束。

Method: 1. 假设规范扇区局域化在膜上，闭弦轴子KK激发态可在暗维度体空间传播；2. 计算轴子塔与标准模型等离子体的冻结产生过程，确定暗物质丰度参数空间；3. 分析多塔之间的能量碎片化与再分配机制；4. 开发连续极限方法将塔碎片化问题约化为积分求解。

Result: 1. 轴子衰变常数在10^12 GeV ≲ f_a ≲ 10^16 GeV、重加热温度在5 MeV ≲ T_RH ≲ O(1) GeV范围内，引力子和轴子塔的冻结产生可提供全部暗物质；2. 当存在N≫1个轴子塔时，任一衰变塔注入标准模型的可见能量被稀释了N倍，从而规避了宇宙学约束；3. 开发的连续方法成功将碎片化问题简化为求积运算。

Conclusion: 暗维度与轴子宇宙的结合提供了一个优雅的框架：不仅通过冻结产生机制自然生成暗物质，还利用多塔能量稀释效应规避了对暗维度暗物质最强的宇宙学约束，显著增强了该场景的唯象可行性。

Abstract: We explore the cosmological implications of combining dark dimension scenarios with an axiverse. If gauge sectors are realized on branes, towers of Kaluza-Klein (KK) excitations of closed string axions can propagate through the dark dimension in addition to the tower of graviton excitations. This modifies cosmology in two ways. First, if any of these axion towers interact with the standard model (SM) plasma, they can significantly alter the freeze-in production of the cosmological abundance of tower states. Freeze-in to graviton and axion towers can provide all of dark matter (DM) for an axion decay constant in $10^{12} \text{ GeV }\lesssim f_a\lesssim 10^{16}\,\text{ GeV }$ and reheating temperatures $5\text{ MeV }\lesssim T_{\rm RH} \lesssim O(1)\,\text{ GeV }$. Second, different towers fragment into each other and redistribute energy; each tower's fraction of energy at late times is fixed by their interactions. If there are $N\gg1$ axion towers, the energy visibly injected into the SM by any decaying tower is diluted by a factor of $N$. This suppression offers a simple realization of how dark dimension dark matter can avoid strong cosmological constraints which rule out the simplest models. In the process of this exploration we develop a continuum approach to evaluating tower fragmentation which offers insight and aids numerical calculations by reducing the problem to quadrature.

</details>


### [209] [The Very Nearly Right Theory of Flavor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27315)
*Nima Arkani-Hamed,Carolina Figueiredo,Lawrence J. Hall,Claudio Andrea Manzari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用"九链纹理"（nine-link textures）参数化 Yukawa 矩阵，发现 CP 破坏相位自然聚集在 π/8 的倍数附近，由此定义的"Yukawa 三角形"在领头阶与幺正三角形重合，解释了其角度为何接近 (π/2, π/8, 3π/8)，并给出了可检验的精确偏离预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKM 幺正三角形的角度 (α, β, γ) 经验上非常接近 (π/2, π/8, 3π/8)——π 的简单分数，强烈暗示味物理与自发 CP 破坏之间存在深层联系。然而，由于幺正三角形是 Yukawa 矩阵的复杂函数，一直难以将这一经验观测与具体的味理论联系起来。本文旨在建立一个直接的桥梁。

Method: 作者采用"九链纹理"方案：假设上型和下型夸克的 Yukawa 矩阵 Yu,d 均为满秩、共含九个非零矩阵元，且仅有一个 CP 破坏相位。在该十维参数空间中，用所有此类纹理拟合味物理数据（夸克质量与 CKM 混合参数）。进一步，由 Yu,d 的矩阵元自然定义一个"Yukawa 三角形"，并在小味参数展开下证明其在领头阶等同于幺正三角形。

Result: 拟合结果显示，CP 相位的取值紧密聚集在 π/8 的倍数附近。Yukawa 三角形在领头阶与幺正三角形相同，解释了为何 (α, β, γ) 接近 (π/2, π/8, 3π/8)。但超出领头阶后两个三角形出现差异，从而给出对幺正三角形角度的精确偏离预言，这些偏离可由下一代实验测量来确认或排除。此外，九链纹理的稀疏性及行列式自然为实数，结合自发 CP 破坏，可为强 CP 问题提供解决方案。

Conclusion: 九链纹理为味物理提供了一个简洁而富有预言力的框架，成功解释了幺正三角形角度近似为 π 的简单分数这一经验事实，并给出了可在未来实验中检验的精确偏离。该框架还自然地指向强 CP 问题的解决路径。

Abstract: A striking empirical observation about the CKM matrix is that the angles of the unitarity triangle $(α, β, γ)$ are very close to $(π/2, π/8, 3 π/8) $, simple fractions of $π$ that are suggestive of an underlying theory linking flavor and spontaneous CP violation. However, relating this empirical observation to an underlying theory of flavor is challenging, since the unitarity triangle is a complicated function of the Yukawa matrices. In this letter we present a simple picture for the Yukawas where this direct link is possible. We begin by parametrizing the ten-dimensional space of flavor data via "nine-link textures", full-rank $Y_{u,d}$ matrices with a total of nine non-zero entries, with a single CP violating phase. Fitting the ten parameters of all such textures to the flavor data reveals a wonderful surprise: the CP phases cluster tightly around multiples of $π/8$! This happens because the entries of $Y_{u,d}$ naturally define a "Yukawa triangle", in most cases identical to the unitarity triangle at leading order in small flavor parameters. Most interestingly, these two triangles are not the same beyond leading order, yielding precise predictions for $(α, β, γ)$ with calculable deviation from $(π/2, π/8, 3 π/8)$, which can be decisively excluded or strongly confirmed by the next generation of experimental measurements of the angles. The 9-link textures are sparse and their determinants are naturally real, which taken together with spontaneous CP violation can resolve the strong CP problem.

</details>


### [210] [Magnetic dipole moments as probes of doubly-bottom molecular pentaquarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27653)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD光锥求和规则，系统研究了三种双底夸克分子态五夸克（$B\Sigma_b$、$B\Sigma_b^*$、$B^*\Sigma_b$）的磁偶极矩，发现其强烈依赖于内部自旋结构和夸克组分，为实验鉴别提供了可检验的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双底五夸克态的内部结构尚不清楚，需要寻找能灵敏区分不同分子态构型的可观测量。磁偶极矩作为强子内部电磁结构的敏感探针，可为鉴别含重味五夸克态的组态和自旋结构提供关键信息。

Method: 在QCD光锥求和规则（QCD Light-Cone Sum Rules）框架下，结合光子分布振幅（photon distribution amplitudes），对量子数为 $J^P=1/2^-$ 和 $3/2^-$ 的 $B\Sigma_b$、$B\Sigma_b^*$ 及 $B^*\Sigma_b$ 分子态进行磁偶极矩计算。

Result: 结果表明磁偶极矩对内部自旋结构和夸克组分高度敏感：(1) $B\Sigma_b$ 构型中轻夸克贡献主导；(2) $B\Sigma_b^*$ 的磁矩主要由重味底夸克决定；(3) $B^*\Sigma_b$ 分子态中轻、重夸克扇区呈建设性贡献，磁偶极矩显著增强。

Conclusion: 磁偶极矩是探测分子型双底五夸克内部结构的灵敏探针，不同分子态构型的磁矩特征差异显著，可为未来实验测量和态鉴别提供明确的理论预言。

Abstract: We investigate the magnetic dipole moments of doubly-bottom pentaquark states with spin-parities $J^P=\tfrac{1}{2}^-$ and $\tfrac{3}{2}^-$, interpreted as hadronic molecules in the $BΣ_b$, $BΣ_b^{*}$, and $B^{*}Σ_b$ configurations. The analysis is performed within the framework of QCD light-cone sum rules employing photon distribution amplitudes. Our results demonstrate a strong sensitivity of the magnetic dipole moments to the internal spin structure and quark composition of the states. In particular, the light-quark sector provides the dominant contribution in the $BΣ_b$ configuration, while the magnetic moment of $BΣ_b^{*}$ is largely governed by the heavy bottom quark. For the $B^{*}Σ_b$ molecular state, both light and heavy sectors contribute constructively, leading to a significantly enhanced magnetic dipole moment. These findings indicate that magnetic dipole moments constitute a sensitive probe of the internal structure of molecular-type doubly-bottom pentaquarks and provide testable predictions for future experimental studies.

</details>


### [211] [Systematic derivation of the Boltzmann equation for electroweak baryogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28321)
*Motoi Endo,Yushi Mura,Tenta Tsuji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在场论框架下系统推导了电弱重子生成中的玻尔兹曼方程，通过逐阶求解Kadanoff-Baym方程并纳入CP破缺气泡壁背景的梯度效应，得到了描述等离子体中准粒子输运的玻尔兹曼方程，推广了以往的处理方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弱重子生成需要一个可靠且系统的方法来描述CP破缺气泡壁背景中粒子的非平衡输运。以往的场论推导存在近似不够系统或忽略自能修正等问题，需要建立一个更透明、更普适的推导框架，统一适用于多味玻色子和费米子系统。

Method: 作者从Kadanoff-Baym方程出发，将CP破缺气泡壁背景的梯度效应逐阶纳入并代数求解。随后施加适用于电弱重子生成的近似与假设，提取解的壳上（on-shell）部分来描述等离子体中传播的准粒子态。在此基础上推导味对角准粒子模式的玻尔兹曼方程，同时忽略描述不同味模式间量子相干性的非对角分量。整个过程保留了自能修正。

Result: 得到了描述味对角准粒子模式的玻尔兹曼方程，该方程在忽略自能修正时与已知的玻尔兹曼方程一致，验证了推导的正确性。该框架适用于多味玻色子与费米子系统，具有系统性和透明性。

Conclusion: 该工作为电弱重子生成中的非平衡输运提供了一个系统、普适且透明的场论推导框架，通过保留自能修正推广了以往的场论处理，并可直接应用于多味玻色子和费米子系统。

Abstract: We derive the Boltzmann equation for electroweak baryogenesis within a field-theoretical framework. We first algebraically solve the Kadanoff--Baym equations by incorporating the effects of gradients of the CP-violating bubble-wall background order by order. We then apply approximations and assumptions appropriate for electroweak baryogenesis and identify the on-shell parts of the solutions, which describe quasiparticle states propagating in a plasma. Using these on-shell solutions, we derive the Boltzmann equations for flavor-diagonal quasiparticle modes, while neglecting the off-diagonal components describing quantum coherence between different flavor modes. Our derivation generalizes previous field-theoretical treatments by retaining self-energy corrections, and our results are consistent with the known Boltzmann equations when these corrections are neglected. This framework provides a systematic and transparent derivation applicable to multi-flavor bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [212] [Why 4D? Spontaneous Dimensional Selection from Gauge Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28615)
*Wen Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 作者提出杨-米尔斯耦合常数在四维时是边际相关的这一临界性质，可自然筛选出四维宏观时空维度，并通过一个具体的赛道模型验证了这一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索为何我们观测到的宏观时空恰好是四维的。作者注意到杨-米尔斯耦合常数在低于四维时相关、四维时边际相关、高于四维时无关，这一维度依赖性可能成为动力学选择四维时空的自然机制。

Method: 采用额外维场景中常用的赛道（racetrack）模型，但不预设非紧致的四维时空。通过规范耦合临界性诱导的超对称破缺和超引力效应，在良好控制的框架下实现radion（额外维尺度模量）的稳定化，从而使四维时空自发涌现。

Result: 在不预先指定四维时空的前提下，模型自发实现了四维宏观时空——通过radion稳定化机制，额外维被紧致化，四维时空作为低能有效理论自然出现。

Conclusion: 杨-米尔斯理论的临界维数性质可以为四维时空的动力学选择提供一种可行的理论机制，该机制在赛道模型中得到了具体验证。

Abstract: Noting that the Yang--Mills coupling is relevant below four dimensions, marginal in four dimensions, and irrelevant above four dimensions, I propose that this criticality can lead to the selection of four macroscopic dimensions. As a proof of principle, I present a concrete racetrack model of the type commonly studied in extra-dimensional scenarios, but without assuming a noncompact 4D spacetime. I find, within a well-controlled model without specifying the 4D, that 4D spacetime is spontaneously realized through radion stabilization via gauge-criticality-induced supersymmetry breaking and supergravity effects.

</details>

### [213] [Semi-supervised Hopfield model: Theoretical and Numerical results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28173)
*Linda Albanese,Andrea Ladiana,Andrea Lepre*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文为半监督Hebbian学习建立了统计力学理论框架，以Hopfield网络为模型，提出监督与无监督Hebbian核的凸组合突触耦合方案，通过信号噪声分析、Guerra插值法和本征通道分解，导出了复本对称淬灭压力及完整相图，证明混合策略优于纯策略，且混合参数λ为学习超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习实践中，全标注数据集获取昂贵且耗时，而无标注数据可批量自动采集。半监督学习是应对这种不对称性的标准方案，但半监督Hebbian学习的统计力学理论一直缺失。本文旨在填补这一空白，为Hopfield网络建立完整的半监督学习理论框架。

Method: 作者首先规定突触耦合为监督与无监督Hebbian核的凸组合（混合参数λ∈[0,1]），两者由相同原型构建。随后进行信号-噪声分析，得到一步Mattis磁化强度和稳定检索所需的最小数据集规模（学习阈值）。接着利用Guerra插值技术，通过特定的本征通道分解处理监督与无监督通道产生的关联无序，在高存储区制下推导出复本对称（Replica Symmetric）淬灭压力。最后通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟验证所有解析结果。

Result: （1）导出的相图表明，混合策略（λ∈(0,1)）在检索性能上优于纯监督（λ=1）和纯无监督（λ=0）两种协议。（2）淬灭压力关于λ是凸函数，因此热力学无法选择内部混合点，λ本质上是一个学习超参数，需由实践者设定。（3）所有解析结论均得到蒙特卡洛模拟的验证。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了Hopfield网络下半监督Hebbian学习的统计力学理论。核心结论是：监督与无监督信号的凸组合在相图层面优于任一极端策略，但混合参数λ不由热力学自发选定，而需作为超参数人为调整。该框架为理解半监督学习中的统计物理机制提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: In the daily practice of Machine Learning, fully labeled datasets are a luxury: labels demand expensive and time-consuming human annotation, whereas raw, unlabeled data can be harvested automatically and in bulk. Semi-supervised learning, where the network jointly exploits the few labeled and the many unlabeled examples at its disposal, is the standard answer to this asymmetry, yet a statistical mechanical theory of semi-supervised Hebbian learning is still lacking. In this paper we fill this gap for the Hopfield network: we prescribe a synaptic coupling given by the convex combination, weighted by a mixing parameter λin [0,1], of the supervised and unsupervised Hebbian kernels built from the same archetypes, and we solve for the emergent computational capabilities of the resulting network. A signal-to-noise analysis yields the one-step Mattis magnetization and the learning threshold, i.e. the minimum dataset size for stable retrieval. Using Guerra's interpolation, we then derive the Replica Symmetric quenched pressure in the high-storage regime, treating the correlated disorder generated by the supervised and unsupervised channels through a particular eigen-channel decomposition. The resulting phase diagram shows that a mixed strategy outperforms both pure protocols. Finally, we prove that the quenched pressure is convex in λ, so thermodynamics cannot select an interior mixture: λis therefore a learning hyperparameter. All the analytical findings are successfully checked against extensive Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Performance Benchmarking: Software for the Density Matrix Renormalization Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28369)
*Per Sehlstedt,Paolo Bientinesi,Lars Karlsson*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向性能的DMRG算法基准测试框架，并对8种实现进行了系统评估，揭示了不同实现之间以及同一实现不同参数配置下可高达两个数量级的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMRG算法有超过50种软件实现，但现有的性能评估缺乏统一标准，要么过于不完整，要么关注其他目标而非直接的性能比较，导致用户和开发者难以在实现之间进行有意义的对比和选择。

Method: 作者设计了一个面向性能的基准测试框架，涵盖多种参数设置、优化策略和实现特定功能的系统化评估方法，并对8种DMRG实现进行了定量性能测试。

Result: 不同实现在对齐参数后性能差异显著（某些情况下高达两个数量级）；同一实现在不同参数配置下也表现出显著性能差异。系统评估揭示了并非显而易见的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 严格的性能评估具有重要价值，该框架和结果可为用户和开发者提供决策依据，推动构建更高效的科学软件。

Abstract: The performance of scientific software often determines the scale of problems that can be solved in practice. As multiple implementations of the same algorithm emerge, systematic evaluation is needed to compare their strengths and limitations. The density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) algorithm, widely used to study quantum systems, has over 50 software implementations. These implementations vary in multiple aspects that can strongly affect performance. However, despite the need, performance evaluations of these implementations are scarce and lack a consistent standard; many existing evaluations are either too incomplete to enable meaningful comparisons or focus on objectives other than direct performance comparisons, thereby limiting understanding of how the implementations compare. Here, we present a performance-oriented benchmarking framework to facilitate meaningful comparisons of DMRG implementations, and we apply it to quantify the performance of eight implementations, highlighting similarities and differences among them. Furthermore, we examine multiple parameter settings, optimization strategies, and implementation-specific features to demonstrate how parameter configuration can affect performance and how systematic evaluation can reveal non-obvious trade-offs. The results show significant performance differences, up to two orders of magnitude in some cases, not only between different implementations when aligning parameters, but also within the same implementation when comparing different parameter configurations. Hence, our results demonstrate the significant value and insight that can be gained from conducting rigorous performance evaluations. Using our results and framework as a starting point, more rigorous benchmarking will ultimately help users and developers make informed decisions and support future development efforts to build better, more efficient software.

</details>


### [215] [Fast and Accurate Foundation Models for Equivariant Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28461)
*Seán R. Kavanagh,Chuin Wei Tan,Menghang Wang,Marc L. Descoteaux,Gabriel de Miranda Nascimento,Ulrik Unneberg,Laura Zichi,Francesco Libbi,Norma Rivano,Austin Glover,Vivek Bharadwaj,Anders Johansson,William C. Witt,Albert Musaelian,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了等变机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）在超大数据集下面临的推理速度、训练速度与精度之间的权衡问题，提出了基于NequIP和Allegro架构的系列基础势模型，在保持领先精度的同时实现了优异的推理速度与可扩展性，并指出提升材料发现模型精度的关键在于数据多样性及过渡金属化合物势能面的一致性描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用MLIP基础模型虽已在计算材料科学中广泛应用，但下游任务（如分子动力学）对推理速度、训练速度及精度均提出高要求。现有等变架构虽在数据效率上占优，但在超大数据集场景下数据效率不再关键，此时等变架构能否同时满足速度与精度目标仍是一个待解问题。

Method: 作者以NequIP和Allegro等变MLIP架构为基础，对训练基础设施进行加速优化以支持超大规模数据集上的高效训练；系统考察等变模型在超大数据集下推理速度、训练可扩展性与精度之间的权衡空间，并在多个社区基准上进行验证，包括材料发现、热导率预测、近平衡力学与热力学性质等任务。

Result: 提出的系列基础势在NequIP和Allegro架构上实现了领先的推理速度和强可扩展性，同时在材料发现、热导率预测及近平衡性质等多类基准上保持优异精度。NequIP基础设施的加速优化使超大规模数据集上高精度基础势的训练计算成本显著降低。研究还发现，提升材料发现任务精度的关键瓶颈在于数据集的多样性以及对过渡金属化合物势能面的一致、准确描述。

Conclusion: 等变MLIP架构可以通过针对性优化克服推理速度与训练可扩展性瓶颈，在超大规模数据集场景下同时实现高速度与高精度。未来材料发现模型的改进应更多聚焦于数据集的多样性与过渡金属化学空间的描述质量，而非仅依赖模型架构的改进。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have emerged as a transformative tool for computational materials science and chemistry, with universal potentials trained on large and diverse datasets now routinely deployed as 'foundation models' for downstream fine-tuning in targeted chemical spaces. Many scientific applications of the resulting models, such as molecular dynamics (MD), require high inference and training speeds as well as accuracy. In this work, we examine the limits of equivariant MLIPs, which directly encode physical symmetries in model architectures, to achieve these competing targets -- particularly in the regime of extremely large datasets where data efficiency is less critical. We show how this trade-off can be addressed, and present a family of foundation potentials in the NequIP and Allegro equivariant MLIP architectures which achieve leading inference speeds and strong scalability as well as excellent accuracies across a range of community benchmarks -- spanning materials discovery, thermal conductivity prediction, and near-equilibrium mechanical and thermodynamic properties. Accelerations implemented within the NequIP infrastructure now permit training of high-accuracy foundation potentials on ultra-large datasets with dramatically reduced computational cost. Alongside, we show that efforts to improve model accuracy for materials discovery should focus on dataset diversity and improved, consistent descriptions of transition metal compound energy surfaces.
